~~~python
import importlib.util, copy, math, json, random, subprocess, sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
FLDB_PATH=str(HERE/'fldb_work.py')
spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('fldb', FLDB_PATH)
m=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec); sys.modules[spec.name]=m; spec.loader.exec_module(m)

RX=[('a1','m2'),('m2','x')]
LX=dict(ante='a1',anchor=('m2','x'),read='x')

class ProbabilityOnFLDB:
    """R&D conservative probability augment over one real FLDB object.
    Adds only binding sediment, probability-organization wiring, event records and readers.
    It never writes Law/Degree/Probability target labels or FLDB g/d/rep/Om/pathdyn.
    """
    def __init__(self,F,seed=0,bind_beta=0.30,bind_decay=0.01,bind_thr=1.0,bind_max=20.0):
        self.F=F
        self.rng=random.Random(seed)
        self.dims={
            'A':dict(R=tuple(m.R1),L=m.L1,path=('a1','m1','c'),entry=('a1','m1')),
            'X':dict(R=tuple(RX),L=LX,path=('a1','m2','x'),entry=('a1','m2')),
        }
        self.bind={d:0.0 for d in self.dims}
        self.bind_rep={d:[] for d in self.dims}
        self.bind_beta=bind_beta; self.bind_decay=bind_decay; self.bind_thr=bind_thr; self.bind_max=bind_max
        self.gen_gate={d:1.0 for d in self.dims}
        self.run_id=f'prob-fldb-{id(self.F)}'; self.branch_id='actual'; self.protocol='ProbRND-1'
        self.events=[]
        self.authoritative_ops=[]
        self.protocol_history=[dict(version=self.protocol, status='frozen', seq=0)]
        self.op_seq=0


    def do_gen_gate(self,dim,value,source='probability-run-wiring'):
        """Authoritative probability-side runtime operation.
        Changes only A_Prob generation wiring; never writes Rel/Probability/D/B/R.
        Every mutation is recorded with run/branch/protocol/source and before/after.
        """
        if dim not in self.gen_gate:
            raise KeyError(dim)
        if value < 0:
            raise ValueError('gen gate must be nonnegative')
        before=float(self.gen_gate[dim])
        self.gen_gate[dim]=float(value)
        self.op_seq+=1
        rec=dict(op_id=self.op_seq,operator='GenGate',dim=dim,before=before,after=float(value),
                 run_id=self.run_id,branch_id=self.branch_id,protocol=self.protocol,
                 source=source,evidence_key=(self.run_id,'op',self.op_seq),authoritative=True)
        self.authoritative_ops.append(rec)
        return rec

    def audit_authoritative_ops(self):
        """L2-side audit: every probability wiring mutation must be a declared authoritative op."""
        for r in self.authoritative_ops:
            if not (r.get('authoritative') is True and r.get('operator')=='GenGate' and
                    r.get('run_id')==self.run_id and r.get('branch_id')==self.branch_id and
                    r.get('protocol')==self.protocol and r.get('source')):
                return False
        return True

    # ----- physical probability augment -----
    def _bind_decay_step(self):
        for d in self.bind:
            self.bind[d]=max(0.0,self.bind[d]-self.bind_decay)
    def _bind_sediment(self,d,p=1.0):
        self.bind[d]=min(self.bind_max,self.bind[d]+self.bind_beta*p*(1-self.bind[d]/self.bind_max))
        self.bind_rep[d].append(self.F.t_sed)
    def _law(self,d,F=None):
        F=F or self.F
        D=self.dims[d]
        return bool(m.judge(F,D['L'],list(D['R']),['a1'])[0])
    def V(self,F=None):
        return tuple(d for d in self.dims if self._law(d,F))
    def D(self,F=None): return len(self.V(F))>=2
    def B(self,F=None):
        # Binding sediment is probability-side physical state; for deep-copy F counterfactuals,
        # it is held fixed because the rate-bearing diagnostic varies generation, not binding history.
        return self.D(F) and all(self.bind[d]>=self.bind_thr for d in self.dims)
    def raw_weights(self,F=None,gates=None):
        F=F or self.F; gates=gates or self.gen_gate
        out=[]
        for d in self.V(F):
            e=self.dims[d]['entry']
            k=F.kappa(e)
            if not math.isfinite(k) or k<=-1e8: w=0.0
            else: w=math.exp(k/F.T)*max(0.0,gates.get(d,0.0))
            out.append((d,w))
        return tuple(out)
    @staticmethod
    def _normalize(w):
        tot=sum(v for _,v in w)
        if tot<=0: return tuple((d,0.0) for d,_ in w)
        return tuple((d,v/tot) for d,v in w)
    def Rel(self,F=None,gates=None): return self._normalize(self.raw_weights(F,gates))
    @staticmethod
    def _same_rel(r0,r1,tol=1e-12):
        if set(dict(r0))!=set(dict(r1)): return False
        return all(abs(dict(r0)[k]-dict(r1)[k])<=tol for k in dict(r0))
    def minsuff(self):
        from itertools import combinations
        if not self.B(): return ()
        base=self.Rel(); dims=list(self.V())
        # Sealed probability semantics: the all-zero relation has the empty set as the
        # inclusion-minimal sufficient generation group.  That does NOT make R true:
        # no formal Law-bearing witness can intersect the empty generation group.
        if all(abs(w)<=1e-15 for _,w in base):
            return ((),)
        MS=[]
        for k in range(1,len(dims)+1):
            for S in combinations(dims,k):
                if any(set(T)<=set(S) for T in MS): continue
                gates={d:(self.gen_gate[d] if d in S else 0.0) for d in dims}
                if self._same_rel(base,self.Rel(gates=gates)):
                    MS.append(S)
        return tuple(MS)
    def R(self):
        ms=self.minsuff()
        return bool(self.B() and ms and any(len(S)>0 for S in ms))
    def Probability(self): return self.D() and self.B() and self.R()
    def snapshot(self):
        return dict(t_run=self.F.t_run,t_sed=self.F.t_sed,V=self.V(),D=self.D(),B=self.B(),R=self.R(),
                    Probability=self.Probability(),Rel=self.Rel(),MinSuff=self.minsuff(),
                    Bind=tuple((d,round(self.bind[d],12)) for d in sorted(self.bind)))

    def formation_pulse(self,dim,p=1.0):
        self._bind_decay_step()
        route,_=self.F.pulse('a1',p,path=list(self.dims[dim]['path']))
        es=set(zip(route,route[1:]))
        for d,D in self.dims.items():
            if set(D['R'])<=es: self._bind_sediment(d,p)
        return route

    def _record_event(self,pre,post,route,kind,dim=None):
        ev=dict(run_id=self.run_id,branch_id=self.branch_id,protocol=self.protocol,t_run=self.F.t_run,
                kind=kind,dim=dim,route=tuple(route),pre=pre,post=post)
        self.events.append(ev); return ev
    def _execute_path(self,dim,kind,p=1.0):
        pre=self.snapshot()
        self._bind_decay_step()
        route,_=self.F.pulse('a1',p,path=list(self.dims[dim]['path']))
        es=set(zip(route,route[1:]))
        for d,D in self.dims.items():
            if set(D['R'])<=es: self._bind_sediment(d,p)
        post=self.snapshot()
        return pre,post,self._record_event(pre,post,route,kind,dim)
    def pulse_external(self,dim,p=1.0):
        return self._execute_path(dim,'external',p)
    def pulse_org(self,p=1.0):
        pre=self.snapshot(); rel=dict(pre['Rel'])
        choices=[d for d in pre['V'] if rel.get(d,0)>0]
        if not choices: return pre,pre,self._record_event(pre,pre,('a1',),'org-none',None)
        r=self.rng.random()*sum(rel[d] for d in choices); chosen=choices[-1]
        for d in choices:
            r-=rel[d]
            if r<=0: chosen=d; break
        # Decision is made by the probability organization, then executed on the same FLDB medium.
        self._bind_decay_step()
        route,_=self.F.pulse('a1',p,path=list(self.dims[chosen]['path']))
        es=set(zip(route,route[1:]))
        for d,D in self.dims.items():
            if set(D['R'])<=es: self._bind_sediment(d,p)
        post=self.snapshot()
        return pre,post,self._record_event(pre,post,route,'org',chosen)
    def ProbabilityAct(self,ev):
        if ev['kind']!='org': return False
        if not ev['pre']['Probability']: return False
        es=set(zip(ev['route'],ev['route'][1:]))
        hits=[d for d,D in self.dims.items() if set(D['R'])<=es]
        return len(hits)==1 and hits[0]==ev['dim'] and ev['run_id']==self.run_id and ev['protocol']==self.protocol

    def dyn_compare(self,s0,s1,act0=False,act1=False):
        contact=s0['Probability'] or s1['Probability']
        if s0['Probability'] and s1['Probability']:
            actdyn='Start' if ((not act0) and act1) else 'Continue' if (act0 and act1) else 'Stop' if (act0 and (not act1)) else None
        else: actdyn=None
        same_rel=self._same_rel(s0['Rel'],s1['Rel'])
        rate=bool(contact and not same_rel)
        dim=bool(contact and s0['V']!=s1['V'])
        bind=bool(contact and s0['Bind']!=s1['Bind'])
        bear=bool(contact and s0['MinSuff']!=s1['MinSuff'] and same_rel)
        return dict(ActDyn=actdyn,RateDyn=rate,DimStructDyn=dim,BindStructDyn=bind,
                    RateBearingStructDyn=bear,SameRel=same_rel)


def build(seed=0,formation=80):
    F=m.new_operational(seed)
    m._set_loop(F,False)
    P=ProbabilityOnFLDB(F,seed=seed)
    for _ in range(formation):
        P.formation_pulse('A'); P.formation_pulse('X')
    m._set_loop(F,True)
    return P

def state_digest(P):
    return (P.F.dyn_snapshot(),tuple(sorted(P.bind.items())),tuple((d,tuple(v)) for d,v in sorted(P.bind_rep.items())))

def run_tests(seeds=range(10)):
    failures=[]; checks=0
    for seed in seeds:
        P=build(seed)
        def ck(name,cond,detail=None):
            nonlocal checks
            checks+=1
            if not cond: failures.append((seed,name,detail))
        s=P.snapshot()
        ck('both_laws',set(s['V'])=={'A','X'},s)
        ck('qualification',s['Probability'] and s['D'] and s['B'] and s['R'],s)
        ck('minsuff_nonempty',bool(s['MinSuff']),s['MinSuff'])
        # reader noncausal
        d0=state_digest(P); [P.snapshot() for _ in range(20)]; d1=state_digest(P)
        ck('reader_noncausal',d0==d1)
        # external event does not count as ProbabilityAct
        _,_,evx=P.pulse_external('A'); ck('external_not_act',not P.ProbabilityAct(evx),evx)
        # endogenous organization event counts
        _,_,evo=P.pulse_org(); ck('org_act',P.ProbabilityAct(evo),evo)
        # carrier-only world: freeze sediment, then execute a real unrelated field pulse.
        # It advances the frozen Degree flow window (evicting one target-flow record) but touches
        # neither A/X pathdyn nor A/X sediment, so the probability generation relation is exactly unchanged.
        s0=P.snapshot(); D0=P.F.DegreeA(); law0=P.F.LawA(); P.F.Freeze()
        P.F.pulse('u',path=['u','y','w'])
        s1=P.snapshot(); D1=P.F.DegreeA(); law1=P.F.LawA()
        pd=P.dyn_compare(s0,s1,False,False)
        ck('carrier_degree_fall',m.cmp_degree(D0,D1)=='Fall' and law0 and law1,(D0,D1,pd))
        ck('carrier_prob_quiet',pd['ActDyn'] is None and not pd['RateDyn'] and not pd['DimStructDyn'] and not pd['BindStructDyn'] and not pd['RateBearingStructDyn'],pd)
        P.F.Thaw()
        # probability relation dynamics via a real FLDB mask intervention on one formal entry while laws remain readable if possible
        # use reversible probability org gate instead: changes A_Prob run wiring, not Law or field sediment.
        s2=P.snapshot(); op=P.do_gen_gate('A',0.2); s3=P.snapshot(); pd2=P.dyn_compare(s2,s3,False,False)
        ck('probability_rate_dyn',pd2['RateDyn'],(s2['Rel'],s3['Rel'],pd2,op))
        ck('authoritative_gen_gate_audit',P.audit_authoritative_ops(),P.authoritative_ops)
        # source-independent qualification: no current pulse required
        ck('idle_qualification_kept',P.Probability())
        # All-zero relation is a Deg_R zero-class boundary, not a qualified probability.
        P0=build(seed+10000); P0.do_gen_gate('A',0.0); P0.do_gen_gate('X',0.0); z=P0.snapshot()
        ck('all_zero_relation_not_probability',dict(z['Rel'])=={'A':0.0,'X':0.0} and z['MinSuff']==((),) and not z['R'] and not z['Probability'],z)
    return dict(seeds=len(list(seeds)),checks=checks,failures=failures,TOTAL_PASS=not failures)

if __name__=='__main__':
    # Upstream must pass first, every run.
    cp=subprocess.run([sys.executable,FLDB_PATH],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=180)
    ok=(cp.returncode==0 and '[FLDB_V1_12_TOTAL] PASS' in cp.stdout)
    print('[UPSTREAM_FULL]', 'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL')
    if not ok:
        print(cp.stdout[-8000:]); print(cp.stderr[-4000:]); raise SystemExit(2)
    r=run_tests(range(3))
    print(json.dumps(r,indent=2,default=str))
    raise SystemExit(0 if r['TOTAL_PASS'] else 1)
~~~

~~~python
import importlib.util, sys, math, random, subprocess, json
from pathlib import Path
HERE=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
BASE=str(HERE/'probability_on_fldb_rnd.py')
spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('pr1',BASE); b=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec); sys.modules[spec.name]=b; spec.loader.exec_module(b)
m=b.m

class ProbabilityOnFLDB(b.ProbabilityOnFLDB):
    """R&D v2: adds a frozen *candidate* carrier registry and current role-wiring.

    The registry is not the current probability organization.  Current BearDim is derived from
    (i) upstream Law qualification, (ii) frozen candidate->formal-direction role mapping, and
    (iii) an authoritative A_Prob physical role-wiring state.  No BearDim/DimStructDyn truth
    value is writable.  A legal bearer handoff may therefore change the current registration
    while the candidate discovery rule and evaluation protocol remain frozen.
    """
    def __init__(self,F,seed=0,**kw):
        super().__init__(F,seed=seed,**kw)
        # Candidate registry is frozen at construction time. A_alt is a physically distinct
        # sedimented Law path to the same formal A successor c; it is not part of current L_P
        # unless its carrier_link is enabled.
        self.carrier_candidates={
            'A_primary':dict(dim='A',R=tuple(m.R1),L=m.L1,path=('a1','m1','c'),entry=('a1','m1'),role='ProbDir:A'),
            'A_alt':dict(dim='A',R=(('a1','m2'),('m2','c')),L=dict(ante='a1',anchor=('m2','c'),read='c'),path=('a1','m2','c'),entry=('a1','m2'),role='ProbDir:A'),
            'X_primary':dict(dim='X',R=tuple(b.RX),L=b.LX,path=('a1','m2','x'),entry=('a1','m2'),role='ProbDir:X'),
        }
        self.candidate_registry_frozen=tuple(sorted((k,v['dim'],v['role'],v['path']) for k,v in self.carrier_candidates.items()))
        self.carrier_link={'A_primary':1.0,'A_alt':0.0,'X_primary':1.0}
        self.protocol='ProbRND-2'
        self.protocol_history=[dict(version=self.protocol,status='frozen',seq=0,
                                    carrier_registry=self.candidate_registry_frozen)]

    def _candidate_law(self,cid,F=None):
        F=F or self.F; C=self.carrier_candidates[cid]
        return bool(m.judge(F,C['L'],list(C['R']),['a1'])[0])

    def BearDimM(self,cid,dim,F=None):
        """Formal bearer read: derives, never writes, current dimension bearing."""
        C=self.carrier_candidates.get(cid)
        if C is None or C['dim']!=dim: return False
        return bool(self.carrier_link.get(cid,0.0)>0 and self._candidate_law(cid,F))

    def current_bearers(self,dim,F=None):
        return tuple(cid for cid,C in self.carrier_candidates.items() if C['dim']==dim and self.BearDimM(cid,dim,F))

    def current_LP(self,F=None):
        return tuple((cid,self.carrier_candidates[cid]['dim'],self.carrier_candidates[cid]['role'])
                     for cid in self.carrier_candidates
                     if self.BearDimM(cid,self.carrier_candidates[cid]['dim'],F))

    def _qstate(self,F=None):
        F=F or self.F
        active={}
        for d in self.dims:
            xs=[]
            for cid,C in self.carrier_candidates.items():
                if C['dim']!=d or self.carrier_link.get(cid,0.0)<=0: continue
                if self._candidate_law(cid,F): xs.append(cid)
            active[d]=tuple(xs)
        V=tuple(d for d in self.dims if active[d])
        D=len(V)>=2
        B=bool(D and all(self.bind[d]>=self.bind_thr for d in self.dims))
        raw=[]
        for d in V:
            vals=[]
            for cid in active[d]:
                e=self.carrier_candidates[cid]['entry']; k=F.kappa(e)
                vals.append(0.0 if (not math.isfinite(k) or k<=-1e8) else math.exp(k/F.T))
            raw.append((d,max(vals,default=0.0)*max(0.0,self.gen_gate.get(d,0.0))))
        rel=self._normalize(tuple(raw))
        zero_rel=all(abs(w)<=1e-15 for _,w in raw)
        MS=[]
        if B and zero_rel:
            # In the sealed formal system the empty set is the minimal sufficient group
            # for [0]_rho, but RateBearWitness fails because no Law-bearing witness can
            # intersect that empty group.  Therefore R=0.
            MS=[()]
        elif B:
            from itertools import combinations
            dims=list(V)
            for k in range(1,len(dims)+1):
                for S in combinations(dims,k):
                    if any(set(T)<=set(S) for T in MS): continue
                    rr=self._normalize(tuple((d,w if d in S else 0.0) for d,w in raw))
                    if self._same_rel(rel,rr): MS.append(S)
        ms=tuple(MS); R=bool(B and (not zero_rel) and ms)
        lp=tuple((cid,self.carrier_candidates[cid]['dim'],self.carrier_candidates[cid]['role'])
                 for cid in self.carrier_candidates if cid in active[self.carrier_candidates[cid]['dim']])
        return dict(active=active,V=V,D=D,B=B,R=R,raw=tuple(raw),Rel=rel,MinSuff=ms,DimOrg=lp,Probability=bool(D and B and R))

    def V(self,F=None): return self._qstate(F)['V']
    def D(self,F=None): return self._qstate(F)['D']
    def B(self,F=None): return self._qstate(F)['B']
    def R(self): return self._qstate()['R']
    def Probability(self): return self._qstate()['Probability']
    def current_bearers(self,dim,F=None): return self._qstate(F)['active'][dim]
    def current_LP(self,F=None): return self._qstate(F)['DimOrg']

    def do_carrier_link(self,cid,value,source='probability-carrier-role-wiring'):
        """Authoritative A_Prob run-layer wiring operation; it does not write BearDim/Probability."""
        if cid not in self.carrier_link: raise KeyError(cid)
        if value not in (0,1,0.0,1.0): raise ValueError('carrier link is binary availability in this R&D spec')
        before=float(self.carrier_link[cid]); self.carrier_link[cid]=float(value); self.op_seq+=1
        rec=dict(op_id=self.op_seq,operator='CarrierLink',candidate=cid,before=before,after=float(value),
                 run_id=self.run_id,branch_id=self.branch_id,protocol=self.protocol,source=source,
                 evidence_key=(self.run_id,'op',self.op_seq),authoritative=True)
        self.authoritative_ops.append(rec); return rec

    def audit_authoritative_ops(self):
        for r in self.authoritative_ops:
            if not (r.get('authoritative') is True and r.get('operator') in {'GenGate','CarrierLink'} and
                    r.get('run_id')==self.run_id and r.get('branch_id')==self.branch_id and
                    r.get('protocol')==self.protocol and r.get('source')):
                return False
        return True

    def _weight_for_dim(self,d,F=None,gates=None):
        F=F or self.F; gates=gates or self.gen_gate
        cids=self.current_bearers(d,F)
        if not cids: return 0.0
        vals=[]
        for cid in cids:
            e=self.carrier_candidates[cid]['entry']; k=F.kappa(e)
            vals.append(0.0 if (not math.isfinite(k) or k<=-1e8) else math.exp(k/F.T))
        # witness multiplicity must not itself increase a formal dimension's rate.
        return max(vals,default=0.0)*max(0.0,gates.get(d,0.0))

    def raw_weights(self,F=None,gates=None):
        if gates is None:
            return self._qstate(F)['raw']
        F=F or self.F; q=self._qstate(F); out=[]
        for d in q['V']:
            vals=[]
            for cid in q['active'][d]:
                e=self.carrier_candidates[cid]['entry']; k=F.kappa(e)
                vals.append(0.0 if (not math.isfinite(k) or k<=-1e8) else math.exp(k/F.T))
            out.append((d,max(vals,default=0.0)*max(0.0,gates.get(d,0.0))))
        return tuple(out)
    def Rel(self,F=None,gates=None):
        return self._normalize(self.raw_weights(F,gates)) if gates is not None else self._qstate(F)['Rel']
    def minsuff(self): return self._qstate()['MinSuff']

    def snapshot(self):
        q=self._qstate()
        return dict(t_run=self.F.t_run,t_sed=self.F.t_sed,V=q['V'],D=q['D'],B=q['B'],R=q['R'],
                    Probability=q['Probability'],Rel=q['Rel'],MinSuff=q['MinSuff'],DimOrg=q['DimOrg'],
                    Bind=tuple((d,round(self.bind[d],12)) for d in sorted(self.bind)),
                    CarrierLink=tuple(sorted(self.carrier_link.items())))

    def execution_candidate(self,dim,F=None):
        cids=self.current_bearers(dim,F)
        if not cids: return None
        # deterministic role-realization choice; frozen ordering, not post-hoc selection.
        return cids[0]

    def execution_path(self,dim,F=None):
        cid=self.execution_candidate(dim,F)
        if cid is None: return tuple(self.dims[dim]['path'])
        return tuple(self.carrier_candidates[cid]['path'])

    def bearer_for_route(self,dim,route):
        es=set(zip(route,route[1:]))
        hits=[]
        for cid in self.current_bearers(dim):
            if set(self.carrier_candidates[cid]['R'])<=es: hits.append(cid)
        return hits[0] if hits else None

    def R_for_route(self,dim,route):
        cid=self.bearer_for_route(dim,route)
        return tuple(self.carrier_candidates[cid]['R']) if cid else tuple(self.dims[dim]['R'])

    def formation_candidate(self,cid,p=1.0):
        C=self.carrier_candidates[cid]
        self._bind_decay_step()
        route,_=self.F.pulse('a1',p,path=list(C['path']))
        es=set(zip(route,route[1:]))
        # dimension binding is sedimented by any registered carrier route for that dimension.
        for d in self.dims:
            if any(set(self.carrier_candidates[x]['R'])<=es for x in self.carrier_candidates if self.carrier_candidates[x]['dim']==d):
                self._bind_sediment(d,p)
        return route

    def _execute_dim_path(self,dim,kind,p=1.0):
        pre=self.snapshot(); self._bind_decay_step(); path=self.execution_path(dim)
        route,_=self.F.pulse('a1',p,path=list(path)); es=set(zip(route,route[1:]))
        for d in self.dims:
            if any(set(self.carrier_candidates[x]['R'])<=es for x in self.carrier_candidates if self.carrier_candidates[x]['dim']==d):
                self._bind_sediment(d,p)
        post=self.snapshot(); return pre,post,self._record_event(pre,post,route,kind,dim)

    def pulse_external(self,dim,p=1.0): return self._execute_dim_path(dim,'external',p)

    def pulse_org(self,p=1.0):
        pre=self.snapshot(); rel=dict(pre['Rel']); choices=[d for d in pre['V'] if rel.get(d,0)>0]
        if not choices: return pre,pre,self._record_event(pre,pre,('a1',),'org-none',None)
        r=self.rng.random()*sum(rel[d] for d in choices); chosen=choices[-1]
        for d in choices:
            r-=rel[d]
            if r<=0: chosen=d; break
        self._bind_decay_step(); route,_=self.F.pulse('a1',p,path=list(self.execution_path(chosen))); es=set(zip(route,route[1:]))
        for d in self.dims:
            if any(set(self.carrier_candidates[x]['R'])<=es for x in self.carrier_candidates if self.carrier_candidates[x]['dim']==d):
                self._bind_sediment(d,p)
        post=self.snapshot(); return pre,post,self._record_event(pre,post,route,'org',chosen)

    def ProbabilityAct(self,ev):
        if ev['kind']!='org' or not ev['pre']['Probability']: return False
        if ev['run_id']!=self.run_id or ev['protocol']!=self.protocol: return False
        return self.bearer_for_route(ev['dim'],ev['route']) is not None

    def dyn_compare(self,s0,s1,act0=False,act1=False):
        d=super().dyn_compare(s0,s1,act0,act1)
        d['DimStructDyn']=bool((s0.get('V')!=s1.get('V')) or (s0.get('DimOrg')!=s1.get('DimOrg')))
        return d


def build(seed=0,formation=80):
    F=m.new_operational(seed); m._set_loop(F,False); P=ProbabilityOnFLDB(F,seed=seed)
    for _ in range(formation): P.formation_pulse('A'); P.formation_pulse('X')
    m._set_loop(F,True); return P

def train_alt_A(P,n=30):
    """Real sedimentation of the alternate A candidate while refreshing established primary A/X."""
    m._set_loop(P.F,False)
    for _ in range(n):
        P.formation_candidate('A_alt')
        P.formation_pulse('A')
        P.formation_pulse('X')
    m._set_loop(P.F,True)
    return P

def run_tests(seeds=range(3)):
    failures=[]; checks=0
    def ck(seed,name,cond,detail=None):
        nonlocal checks; checks+=1
        if not cond: failures.append((seed,name,detail))
    for seed in seeds:
        P=build(seed)
        s=P.snapshot(); ck(seed,'legacy_qualification',s['Probability'] and s['DimOrg']==(('A_primary','A','ProbDir:A'),('X_primary','X','ProbDir:X')),s)
        _,_,e=P.pulse_org(); ck(seed,'legacy_org_act',P.ProbabilityAct(e),e)
        # Train a second *candidate* while it remains outside current L_P.
        train_alt_A(P,30)
        ck(seed,'alt_law_exists_but_not_current_bearer',P._candidate_law('A_alt') and not P.BearDimM('A_alt','A'),(P._candidate_law('A_alt'),P.current_LP()))
        q0=P.Probability(); lp0=P.current_LP(); reg0=P.candidate_registry_frozen
        # Legal handoff: add alternate current role first, then remove primary; no interval lacks A bearing.
        o1=P.do_carrier_link('A_alt',1); qmid=P.Probability(); lpmid=P.current_LP()
        o2=P.do_carrier_link('A_primary',0); q1=P.Probability(); lp1=P.current_LP()
        ck(seed,'handoff_qualification_never_lost',q0 and qmid and q1,(lp0,lpmid,lp1,P.snapshot()))
        ck(seed,'handoff_actual_bearer_replaced',('A_primary','A','ProbDir:A') in lp0 and ('A_alt','A','ProbDir:A') in lp1 and ('A_primary','A','ProbDir:A') not in lp1,(lp0,lp1))
        ck(seed,'candidate_discovery_frozen',reg0==P.candidate_registry_frozen,reg0)
        ck(seed,'authoritative_handoff_ops',P.audit_authoritative_ops() and o1['operator']=='CarrierLink' and o2['operator']=='CarrierLink',P.authoritative_ops[-2:])
        # Replacement itself is formal dimension-organization dynamics, not same-carrier Degree dynamics.
        s0=dict(P.snapshot()); # current post state; reconstruct endpoint snapshots from stored LP below for direct structure check
        ck(seed,'bearhold_primary_fails_across_replacement',not P.BearDimM('A_primary','A'),P.current_bearers('A'))
        # All-zero relation: MinSuff is {empty}, but no rate-bearing witness exists; R and Probability must be 0.
        Z=build(seed+10000); Z.do_gen_gate('A',0.0); Z.do_gen_gate('X',0.0); zs=Z.snapshot()
        ck(seed,'all_zero_relation_zero_class_boundary',dict(zs['Rel'])=={'A':0.0,'X':0.0} and zs['MinSuff']==((),) and not zs['R'] and not zs['Probability'],zs)
    return dict(seeds=len(list(seeds)),checks=checks,failures=failures,TOTAL_PASS=not failures)

if __name__=='__main__':
    cp=subprocess.run([sys.executable,b.FLDB_PATH],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=180)
    ok=(cp.returncode==0 and '[FLDB_V1_12_TOTAL] PASS' in cp.stdout)
    print('[UPSTREAM_FULL]', 'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL')
    if not ok: print(cp.stdout[-8000:]); print(cp.stderr[-4000:]); raise SystemExit(2)
    r=run_tests(range(3)); print(json.dumps(r,indent=2,default=str)); raise SystemExit(0 if r['TOTAL_PASS'] else 1)
~~~

~~~python
import importlib.util, sys, subprocess, json, random, copy, hashlib
from pathlib import Path

HERE=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
PROB_PATH=str(HERE/'probability_on_fldb_rnd_v2.py')
spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('pr',PROB_PATH)
pr=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec); sys.modules[spec.name]=pr; spec.loader.exec_module(pr)
m=pr.m

I_NODES=['sI','gA','gX','tA','tX']
I_EDGES=[('sI','a1'),('c','gA'),('x','gX'),('gA','tA'),('gX','tX')]

class IntentOnFLDB:
    def __init__(self,P,seed=0):
        self.P=P; self.F=P.F; self.rng=random.Random(seed)
        self.source_edge=('sI','a1')
        self.gate={'A':'gA','X':'gX'}
        self.gate_link={'A':('c','gA'),'X':('x','gX')}
        self.tune={'A':('gA','tA'),'X':('gX','tX')}
        self.anchor='IntentAnchor-1'; self.run_id=f'intent-fldb-{id(self.F)}'; self.branch_id='actual'
        self.protocol='IntentRND-1'; self.seq=0; self.events=[]; self.gate_state=None
        # Explicit declared location interface.  It is part of P_I and remains three-valued:
        # True / False / None(=⊥).  The reference construction declares the positive location.
        self.intent_loc_declared=True
        # Frozen IDyn protocol object.  L6 audits this complete object/history, not only a version string.
        self.idyn_protocol={
            'version':'IDynRND-14','status':'frozen','frozen_seq':0,
            'unit_rule':'event-units-closed','delta_src':1,'delta_act':1,
            'carrier_discovery':'frozen-candidate-registry','role_identity':'frozen-role-ids',
            'degree_rule':'Gamma_D-reference','coverage_rule':'branch-local-certificates',
            'attribution_rule':'same-run-branch-anchor-evidence-key'
        }
        self.idyn_protocol_history=[copy.deepcopy(self.idyn_protocol)]
        # Formal/audit interface state. These are readout-side identities and histories;
        # none of them write Intent/Action/Switch/Dyn truth labels or FLDB state.
        self.protocol_history=[dict(version=self.protocol,status='frozen',seq=0)]
        self.coverage={
            'EventCover':True,'ImplCover':True,'SrcChainCover':True,'ProbCertCover':True,
            'DirectionCover':True,'GateRoleCover':True,'TuneRoleCover':True,'BindCover':True,
            'CandidateCover':True,'GateBearerCover':True,'SequenceCover':True,
            'AnchorOrderOrgCover':True,'UpdateCover':True,'QualEventCover':True,
            'CarrierRegisterCover':True,'DegreeCover':True,'PDynCover':True,'ActionWindowCover':True
        }
        self.gate_role_id={'A':'GateRole:A','X':'GateRole:X'}
        self.gate_role_bearers={'GateRole:A':('gA',),'GateRole:X':('gX',)}
        self.gate_role_cover=True
        # Frozen Corr registry is a role relation, not a truth label. Negative pairs are
        # readable only because the full V_I x Gbar_I domain is closed in this reference spec.
        self.corr_registry=frozenset({('A','GateRole:A'),('X','GateRole:X')})
        self.corr_domain_closed=True
        self.tune_role_id={'A':'TuneRole:A','X':'TuneRole:X'}
        self.tune_registry={
            'GateRole:A':({'role_id':'TuneRole:A','bearer_edge':('gA','tA'),'kind':'tune','anchor':'IntentAnchor-1'},),
            'GateRole:X':({'role_id':'TuneRole:X','bearer_edge':('gX','tX'),'kind':'tune','anchor':'IntentAnchor-1'},),
        }
        self.tune_candidate_cover=True
        self.source_role_id='SourceRole:Intent'
        self.source_registry={
            'role_id':self.source_role_id,'bearer_edge':self.source_edge,'inside_boundary':True,
            'anchor':'IntentAnchor-1','prob_org_id':self.P.run_id,
            'continuation_entry':'a1','direction_domain':frozenset(self.P.dims.keys()),
            'external_complete_selector':False,
        }
        self.source_registry_cover=True
        self.system_boundary='IntentBoundary:v1'
        self.intent_ops=[]; self.intent_op_seq=0
        self.source_session_active=None; self.source_session_seq=0; self.source_session_history=[]
    def edge_ready(self,e):
        return e in self.F.E and self.F.Deposited(e) and self.F.Groove(e) and self.F.Avail[e]!=0
    def edge_structural(self,e):
        # Qualification reads established sedimented role structure, not whether a current event can traverse it.
        # Current Avail/Mask belongs to occurrence/action, so temporary stop/recovery must not erase qualification.
        return e in self.F.E and self.F.Deposited(e) and self.F.Groove(e)
    # ----- sealed intent-qualification interfaces (three-valued where coverage matters) -----
    def SrcInternalReadM(self):
        r=self.source_registry
        if not self.source_registry_cover: return None
        return bool(r.get('inside_boundary') and r.get('role_id')==self.source_role_id and self.edge_structural(r.get('bearer_edge')))

    def SrcOrgBindReadM(self):
        r=self.source_registry
        if not self.source_registry_cover: return None
        return bool(r.get('anchor')==self.anchor and r.get('prob_org_id')==self.P.run_id)

    def SrcContinuationReadM(self):
        r=self.source_registry
        if not self.source_registry_cover: return None
        e=r.get('bearer_edge')
        # Structural qualification: current silence/Avail=0 does not erase the established duty.
        # The source continuation must physically enter the probability antecedent a1.
        return bool(self.edge_structural(e) and e[1]==r.get('continuation_entry')=='a1')

    def SrcDomainBindReadM(self):
        r=self.source_registry
        if not self.source_registry_cover: return None
        return bool(frozenset(r.get('direction_domain',()))==frozenset(self.P.dims.keys()) and
                    not r.get('external_complete_selector',False))

    @staticmethod
    def _and3(vals):
        vals=list(vals)
        if any(v is False for v in vals): return False
        if all(v is True for v in vals): return True
        return None

    def SourceDriveM(self):
        internal=self.SrcInternalReadM(); bind=self.SrcOrgBindReadM()
        cont=self.SrcContinuationReadM(); dom=self.SrcDomainBindReadM()
        bear=self._and3((cont,dom))
        value=self._and3((internal,bind,bear))
        return dict(SourceInternal=internal,SourceOrgBind=bind,SrcContinuation=cont,
                    SrcDomainBind=dom,SourceOrgBear=bear,Read=value)

    def gate_roles(self):
        return tuple(sorted(self.gate_role_bearers))

    def CorrReadM(self,dim,gate_role):
        if not self.corr_domain_closed: return None
        if dim not in self.P.dims or gate_role not in self.gate_role_bearers: return None
        return (dim,gate_role) in self.corr_registry

    def PassReadM(self,dim,gate_role):
        if dim not in self.P.dims or gate_role not in self.gate_role_bearers: return None
        # Legal reachability is physical: a current Law-bearing formal dimension ends at c/x,
        # and at least one bearer of the gate-role is connected by an established sedimented edge.
        end={'A':'c','X':'x'}[dim]
        bearers=self.gate_role_bearers[gate_role]
        if not self.gate_role_cover: return None
        return any(self.edge_structural((end,g)) for g in bearers if (end,g) in self.F.E)

    def GateCorrespondenceM(self):
        V=tuple(self.P.dims.keys()); G=self.gate_roles()
        if not (self.corr_domain_closed and self.gate_role_cover):
            return dict(GC1=None,GC2=None,GC3=None,GC4=None,GC5=None,Read=None)
        corr={(v,g):self.CorrReadM(v,g) for v in V for g in G}
        pas={(v,g):self.PassReadM(v,g) for v in V for g in G}
        GC1=all(any(corr[(v,g)] is True for g in G) for v in V)
        GC2=all(sum(corr[(v,g)] is True for g in G)==1 for v in V)
        GC3=all(sum(corr[(v,g)] is True for v in V)==1 for g in G)
        GC4=all(any(corr[(v,g)] is True for v in V) for g in G)
        GC5=all((corr[(v,g)] is not True) or (pas[(v,g)] is True) for v in V for g in G)
        return dict(GC1=GC1,GC2=GC2,GC3=GC3,GC4=GC4,GC5=GC5,Read=all((GC1,GC2,GC3,GC4,GC5)),Corr=corr,Pass=pas)

    def PostGateTuneRoleReadM(self,gate_role,q):
        if not self.tune_candidate_cover: return None
        if gate_role not in self.gate_role_bearers: return None
        e=q.get('bearer_edge')
        gate_nodes=set(self.gate_role_bearers[gate_role])
        return bool(q.get('kind')=='tune' and q.get('anchor')==self.anchor and e in self.F.E and
                    e[0] in gate_nodes and self.edge_structural(e))

    def TuneInterfaceM(self):
        vals={}
        for g in self.gate_roles():
            if g not in self.tune_registry:
                vals[g]=None if not self.tune_candidate_cover else False
                continue
            rs=[self.PostGateTuneRoleReadM(g,q) for q in self.tune_registry[g]]
            if any(v is True for v in rs): vals[g]=True
            elif all(v is False for v in rs): vals[g]=False
            else: vals[g]=None
        value=self._and3(vals.values())
        return dict(per_gate=vals,Read=value)

    def IntentLocM(self):
        v=self.intent_loc_declared
        return True if v is True else False if v is False else None

    def P_IM(self):
        return self._and3((self.IntentLocM(), bool(self.P.Probability())))

    def SourceDrive(self): return self.SourceDriveM()['Read']
    def GateCorrespondence(self): return self.GateCorrespondenceM()['Read']
    def TuneInterface(self): return self.TuneInterfaceM()['Read']
    def Intent(self):
        return self._and3((self.P_IM(), self.SourceDriveM()['Read'], self.GateCorrespondenceM()['Read'], self.TuneInterfaceM()['Read']))
    def qualification(self):
        sd=self.SourceDriveM(); gc=self.GateCorrespondenceM(); ti=self.TuneInterfaceM(); loc=self.IntentLocM(); pi=self.P_IM()
        return dict(P=self.P.Probability(),IntentLoc=loc,P_I=pi,SourceDrive=sd['Read'],GateCorrespondence=gc['Read'],
                    TuneInterface=ti['Read'],Intent=self._and3((pi,sd['Read'],gc['Read'],ti['Read'])),
                    anchor=self.anchor,SourceDetail=sd,GateDetail=gc,TuneDetail=ti)

    def current_package(self):
        """Current implementation package Π_t^I with nested probability package Π_t^P."""
        return dict(run_id=self.run_id,branch_id=self.branch_id,protocol=self.protocol,anchor=self.anchor,
                    source_role=self.source_role_id,gate_roles=dict(self.gate_role_id),
                    tune_roles=dict(self.tune_role_id),probability=dict(run_id=self.P.run_id,
                    branch_id=self.P.branch_id,protocol=self.P.protocol,V=self.P.V(),Rel=self.P.Rel(),
                    MinSuff=self.P.minsuff()))

    def probability_current_certificate(self, pe):
        """Current-occurrence / duty-conformance witness on exactly one probability event.

        This deliberately does NOT call ProbabilityAct(), because sealed IA A2 embeds the
        occurrence and duty-conformance predicates of the same witness rather than the aggregate
        probability-action verdict.
        """
        if pe is None: return None
        pre=pe.get('pre',{}); dim=pe.get('dim'); route=tuple(pe.get('route',()))
        if dim not in self.P.dims: return None
        es=set(zip(route,route[1:]))
        pre_org={cid for cid,d,role in pre.get('DimOrg',()) if d==dim}
        hits=[]
        for cid in pre_org:
            C=self.P.carrier_candidates.get(cid)
            if C and set(C['R'])<=es: hits.append(cid)
        same_run=(pe.get('run_id')==self.P.run_id and pe.get('branch_id')==self.P.branch_id and pe.get('protocol')==self.P.protocol)
        current_occurrence=bool(pe.get('kind')=='org' and pre.get('Probability') is True and same_run and hits)
        minsuff=tuple(pre.get('MinSuff',()))
        current_rate=bool(current_occurrence and any(dim in group for group in minsuff if group))
        eval_bind=bool(same_run and pe.get('t_run') is not None)
        duty=bool(current_occurrence and current_rate and eval_bind)
        return dict(kappa_rho=pre.get('Rel'),MinSuff_rho=minsuff,GenIn=bool(hits),
                    EvalRateBind=eval_bind,CurrentRateRealize=current_rate,
                    CurrentProbabilityOccurrence=current_occurrence,
                    ProbabilityDutyConformance=duty,bearer_hits=tuple(sorted(hits)),
                    nu_cur=(self.P.run_id,pe.get('t_run'),pe.get('protocol')) if same_run else None)

    def CurrentProbabilityOccurrenceM(self,pe):
        c=self.probability_current_certificate(pe)
        return None if c is None else c['CurrentProbabilityOccurrence']

    def ProbabilityDutyConformanceM(self,pe):
        c=self.probability_current_certificate(pe)
        if c is None: return None
        if not c['CurrentProbabilityOccurrence']: return None
        return c['ProbabilityDutyConformance']

    def _source_chains(self, dim, route, pe):
        """Z_src/Z_rho/Z_G/Z_T from the actual route, including explicit covered absences.

        A blocked gate/tune does not erase the upstream real subsegments.  Missing downstream
        occurrences are represented by segment=None plus the frozen finite candidate domain.
        """
        if dim not in self.gate: return None
        gate=self.gate[dim]; gate_edge=self.gate_link[dim]; prob_end=gate_edge[0]
        tune_edge=self.tune[dim]; tune=tune_edge[1]; r=tuple(route); key=(self.run_id,self.seq)
        if 'sI' not in r or 'a1' not in r: return None
        i_src=r.index('sI'); i_p=r.index('a1')
        if not i_src < i_p: return None
        chains={
          'Z_src':dict(role=self.source_role_id,bearer=self.source_edge,segment=r[i_src:i_p+1],actual=(self.source_edge in set(zip(r,r[1:]))),key=key)
        }
        # Probability generation segment reaches the frozen formal direction endpoint c/x.
        if prob_end in r[i_p+1:]:
            i_e=r.index(prob_end,i_p+1)
            chains['Z_rho']=dict(role=f'ProbDir:{dim}',bearer=self.P.bearer_for_route(dim,r),segment=r[i_p:i_e+1],actual=True,key=key)
        else:
            chains['Z_rho']=dict(role=f'ProbDir:{dim}',bearer=None,segment=None,actual=False,
                                 rho_domain=tuple(self.P.dims[dim]['path']),domain_enumerated=True,key=key)
        # Gate pass.
        if gate in r and gate_edge in set(zip(r,r[1:])):
            i_g=r.index(gate)
            chains['Z_G']=dict(role=self.gate_role_id[dim],bearer=gate,segment=r[r.index(prob_end):i_g+1],actual=True,key=key)
        else:
            chains['Z_G']=dict(role=self.gate_role_id[dim],bearer=None,segment=None,actual=False,
                               gate_domain=(gate_edge,),domain_enumerated=True,key=key)
        # Tune entry.
        if gate in r and tune in r[r.index(gate)+1:] and tune_edge in set(zip(r,r[1:])):
            i_g=r.index(gate); i_t=r.index(tune,i_g+1)
            chains['Z_T']=dict(role=self.tune_role_id[dim],bearer=tune_edge,segment=r[i_g:i_t+1],actual=True,key=key)
        else:
            chains['Z_T']=dict(role=self.tune_role_id[dim],bearer=None,segment=None,actual=False,ActualTuneEntrySeg=0,
                               tune_domain=(tune_edge,),domain_enumerated=True,key=key)
        return chains

    def ia_coverage_certificate(self, ev):
        """Coverage certificate assembled from actual records plus frozen finite registries.

        `origin` makes the epistemic source explicit: run-derived fields are proved from this
        event/package; spec-frozen fields are finite-domain declarations of the reference spec.
        No bare `coverage=True` flag can make the certificate pass.
        """
        pe=ev.get('p_event') if isinstance(ev,dict) else None
        cert=self.probability_current_certificate(pe) if pe is not None else None
        chains=ev.get('chains') if isinstance(ev,dict) else None
        req_event=('event_id','evidence_key','run_id','branch_id','protocol','anchor','execution_class','route')
        event_cover=bool(isinstance(ev,dict) and all(k in ev for k in req_event) and ev in self.events)
        pkg=self.current_package()
        impl_cover=bool(pkg.get('run_id')==self.run_id and pkg.get('branch_id')==self.branch_id and
                        pkg.get('protocol')==self.protocol and pkg.get('anchor')==self.anchor and pkg.get('probability'))
        chain_cover=bool(isinstance(chains,dict) and all(k in chains for k in ('Z_src','Z_rho','Z_G','Z_T')) and
                         all((z.get('actual') is True and bool(z.get('segment'))) or
                             (z.get('actual') is False and z.get('segment') is None and z.get('domain_enumerated') is True)
                             for z in chains.values()))
        prob_cover=bool(cert is not None and all(k in cert for k in ('kappa_rho','MinSuff_rho','GenIn','EvalRateBind','CurrentRateRealize','nu_cur')))
        direction_cover=bool(self.P.dims and ev.get('dim') in self.P.dims)
        gate_cover=bool(self.corr_domain_closed and self.gate_role_cover and self.gate_roles() and
                        all(bool(self.gate_role_bearers[g]) for g in self.gate_roles()))
        tune_cover=bool(self.tune_candidate_cover and set(self.tune_registry)==set(self.gate_roles()) and
                        all(len(self.tune_registry[g])>0 for g in self.gate_roles()))
        bind_cover=bool(chain_cover and ev.get('evidence_key') is not None and
                        all(z.get('key')==ev.get('evidence_key') for z in chains.values()))
        items={
            'EventCover':(event_cover,'run-derived'), 'ImplCover':(impl_cover,'run-derived'),
            'SrcChainCover':(chain_cover,'run-derived'), 'ProbCertCover':(prob_cover,'run-derived/upstream-interface'),
            'DirectionCover':(direction_cover,'spec-frozen+event-derived'), 'GateRoleCover':(gate_cover,'spec-frozen'),
            'TuneRoleCover':(tune_cover,'spec-frozen'), 'BindCover':(bind_cover,'run-derived')}
        # IA-M12 is scoped to the failure layers used by this candidate.  A source-only candidate
        # does not require probability/direction/gate/tune positive material merely to close A1 coverage.
        required=('EventCover','ImplCover','SrcChainCover','BindCover') if ev.get('kind')=='source-only' else tuple(items)
        return dict(items=items,required=required,closed=all(items[k][0] for k in required))

    def igs_coverage_certificate(self, prev, cur):
        event_pair=bool(prev in self.events and cur in self.events and prev.get('event_id',0)<cur.get('event_id',0))
        candidate=bool(event_pair and self.corr_domain_closed and self.gate_role_cover)
        bearer=bool(cur.get('gate_bearer_record') is not None and cur.get('gate_after') in
                    {x for xs in self.gate_role_bearers.values() for x in xs} and cur.get('old_gate_exit') is not None)
        sequence=bool(prev.get('sequence_id')==cur.get('sequence_id')==self.run_id and
                      prev.get('event_key') is not None and cur.get('event_key') is not None)
        anchor_order_org=bool(prev.get('anchor')==cur.get('anchor')==self.anchor and
                              prev.get('event_id',0)<cur.get('event_id',0) and prev.get('org_id')==cur.get('org_id'))
        items={'CandidateCover':(candidate,'run-derived+spec-frozen'),
               'GateBearerCover':(bearer,'run-derived+spec-frozen'),
               'SequenceCover':(sequence,'run-derived'),
               'AnchorOrderOrgCover':(anchor_order_org,'run-derived')}
        return dict(items=items,closed=all(v for v,_ in items.values()))

    def ia_conditions(self, ev, overrides=None):
        """IA-M1..M12 interface audit. Conditions are solvability, not object truth."""
        o=overrides or {}; pkg=self.current_package(); pe=ev.get('p_event') if isinstance(ev,dict) else None
        cert=self.probability_current_certificate(pe)
        chains=ev.get('chains') if isinstance(ev,dict) else None
        def C(k,v): return bool(o.get(k,v))
        out={
          'IA-M1':C('IA-M1', isinstance(ev,dict) and bool(ev.get('instance_id')) and ev.get('gamma_frozen') is True),
          'IA-M2':C('IA-M2', isinstance(ev,dict) and ev.get('execution_class') in {'current','replay','offline','qualification-replay'} and ev.get('execution_class')=='current'),
          'IA-M3':C('IA-M3', bool(pkg.get('run_id')) and bool(pkg.get('probability')) and pkg.get('anchor')==self.anchor),
          'IA-M4':C('IA-M4', ev.get('source_role_id')==self.source_role_id and ev.get('system_boundary')==self.system_boundary and ev.get('source_bearer') is not None),
          'IA-M5':C('IA-M5', isinstance(chains,dict) and all(k in chains for k in ('Z_src','Z_rho','Z_G','Z_T')) and
                     all((z.get('actual') is True and bool(z.get('segment'))) or
                         (z.get('actual') is False and z.get('segment') is None and z.get('domain_enumerated') is True)
                         for z in chains.values())),
          'IA-M6':C('IA-M6', cert is not None and cert.get('nu_cur') is not None and pe.get('kind') in {'org','external'}),
          'IA-M7':C('IA-M7', cert is not None and all(k in cert for k in ('kappa_rho','MinSuff_rho','GenIn','EvalRateBind','CurrentRateRealize'))),
          'IA-M8':C('IA-M8', ev.get('dim') in self.P.dims and ev.get('formal_direction')==ev.get('dim')),
          'IA-M9':C('IA-M9', ev.get('dim') in self.gate_role_id and ev.get('gate_role_id')==self.gate_role_id.get(ev.get('dim'))),
          'IA-M10':C('IA-M10', ev.get('dim') in self.tune_role_id and ev.get('tune_role_id')==self.tune_role_id.get(ev.get('dim'))),
          'IA-M11':C('IA-M11', ev.get('evidence_key') is not None and chains is not None and
                     all(z.get('key')==ev.get('evidence_key') for z in chains.values())),
          'IA-M12':C('IA-M12', self.ia_coverage_certificate(ev)['closed'])
        }
        return out

    def A_read3(self, ev, overrides=None):
        cond=self.ia_conditions(ev,overrides)
        missing=[k for k,v in cond.items() if not v]
        if missing: return dict(Read=None,reason=missing[0],conditions=cond)
        r=self.A_read(ev); return dict(**r,reason=None,conditions=cond)

    def igs_conditions(self, prev, cur, overrides=None):
        o=overrides or {}
        def C(k,v): return bool(o.get(k,v))
        ia_cur=self.ia_conditions(cur)
        return {
          'IGS-M1':C('IGS-M1', bool(cur.get('switch_instance_id')) and cur.get('igs_gamma_frozen') is True),
          'IGS-M2':C('IGS-M2', cur.get('seq_domain_frozen') is True and prev.get('event_id',0)<cur.get('event_id',0)),
          'IGS-M3':C('IGS-M3', prev.get('execution_class')=='current' and bool(prev.get('gate_pass_segment'))),
          'IGS-M4':C('IGS-M4', all(ia_cur.get(k,False) for k in ['IA-M1','IA-M2','IA-M3','IA-M4','IA-M5','IA-M6','IA-M7','IA-M8','IA-M9','IA-M11','IA-M12'])),
          'IGS-M5':C('IGS-M5', cur.get('gate_role_id') in set(self.gate_role_id.values())),
          'IGS-M6':C('IGS-M6', cur.get('gate_bearer_record') is not None and cur.get('old_gate_exit') is not None),
          'IGS-M7':C('IGS-M7', prev.get('event_key') is not None and cur.get('event_key') is not None),
          'IGS-M8':C('IGS-M8', prev.get('sequence_id')==cur.get('sequence_id')==self.run_id),
          'IGS-M9':C('IGS-M9', prev.get('anchor')==cur.get('anchor')==self.anchor and prev.get('event_id',0)<cur.get('event_id',0) and prev.get('org_id')==cur.get('org_id')),
          'IGS-M10':C('IGS-M10', self.igs_coverage_certificate(prev,cur)['closed'])
        }

    def switch_read3(self, prev, cur, overrides=None):
        cond=self.igs_conditions(prev,cur,overrides); miss=[k for k,v in cond.items() if not v]
        if miss: return dict(Read=None,reason=miss[0],conditions=cond)
        r=self.switch_read(prev,cur); return dict(**r,reason=None,conditions=cond)

    def source_start(self, source='intent-source-input'):
        """Open a real source-drive session. This records the source process identity; it does not assert SourceDyn."""
        if self.source_session_active is not None:
            return self.source_session_active
        self.source_session_seq += 1
        sid=(self.run_id,'src-session',self.source_session_seq)
        self.source_session_active=sid
        self.intent_op_seq += 1
        rec=dict(op_id=self.intent_op_seq,operator='SourceStart',session_id=sid,t_run=self.F.t_run,
                 run_id=self.run_id,branch_id=self.branch_id,protocol=self.protocol,source=source,
                 evidence_key=(self.run_id,'iop',self.intent_op_seq),authoritative=True)
        self.intent_ops.append(rec); self.source_session_history.append(rec)
        return sid

    def source_stop(self, source='intent-source-input'):
        """Close the current source-drive session; absence between stop and next start is explicit, not inferred from missing events."""
        sid=self.source_session_active
        if sid is None: return None
        self.intent_op_seq += 1
        rec=dict(op_id=self.intent_op_seq,operator='SourceStop',session_id=sid,t_run=self.F.t_run,
                 run_id=self.run_id,branch_id=self.branch_id,protocol=self.protocol,source=source,
                 evidence_key=(self.run_id,'iop',self.intent_op_seq),authoritative=True)
        self.intent_ops.append(rec); self.source_session_history.append(rec); self.source_session_active=None
        return rec

    def _admissible_path(self, route):
        """Intent-side legal execution wrapper: an explicit planned route may not bypass FLDB Avail.
        Returns the longest prefix whose edges are currently available."""
        r=list(route)
        if not r: return r
        out=[r[0]]
        for a,b in zip(r,r[1:]):
            e=(a,b)
            if e not in self.F.Avail or not self.F.Avail[e]:
                break
            out.append(b)
        return out

    def do_avail(self, edge, value, source='intent-admissible-availability'):
        edge=tuple(edge)
        if edge not in I_EDGES: raise KeyError(('NotIntentEdge',edge))
        if edge not in self.F.Avail: raise KeyError(edge)
        before=self.F.Avail[edge]; self.F.SetAvail(edge,value); self.intent_op_seq+=1
        rec=dict(op_id=self.intent_op_seq,operator='Avail',edge=edge,before=before,after=value,
                 run_id=self.run_id,branch_id=self.branch_id,protocol=self.protocol,source=source,
                 evidence_key=(self.run_id,'iop',self.intent_op_seq),authoritative=True)
        self.intent_ops.append(rec); return rec

    def audit_intent_ops(self):
        legal_ops={'SourceStart','SourceStop','Avail'}
        return all(r.get('authoritative') and r.get('operator') in legal_ops and
                   r.get('run_id')==self.run_id and r.get('branch_id')==self.branch_id and
                   r.get('protocol')==self.protocol and r.get('source')
                   for r in self.intent_ops)

    def org_gate_event(self, force_dim=None, p=1.0):
        """Real organization-current event through A1-A3, deliberately stopping at the gate (A4=0)."""
        preI=self.qualification(); preP=self.P.snapshot(); dim=force_dim if force_dim is not None else self._choose_dim()
        route=('sI',)+tuple(self.P.execution_path(dim))+(self.gate[dim],)
        self.P._bind_decay_step(); real_route,_=self.F.pulse('sI',p,path=self._admissible_path(route)); es=set(zip(real_route,real_route[1:]))
        for d,D in self.P.dims.items():
            if set(D['R'])<=es: self.P._bind_sediment(d,p)
        self.seq+=1; before_gate=self.gate_state; after_gate=self.gate[dim]; handoff=(before_gate is not None and before_gate!=after_gate); self.gate_state=after_gate
        pe=dict(run_id=self.P.run_id,branch_id=self.P.branch_id,protocol=self.P.protocol,t_run=self.F.t_run,
                kind='org',dim=dim,route=tuple(real_route),pre=preP,post=self.P.snapshot())
        ekey=(self.run_id,self.seq)
        # Actual route stops at the gate. Z_T is therefore an explicit missing occurrence, not a fabricated segment.
        chains=self._source_chains(dim,real_route,pe)
        ev=dict(event_id=self.seq,evidence_key=ekey,event_key=ekey,instance_id=('IAct',ekey),gamma_frozen=True,
                run_id=self.run_id,branch_id=self.branch_id,protocol=self.protocol,anchor=self.anchor,execution_class='current',
                kind='org',dim=dim,formal_direction=dim,route=tuple(real_route),preI=preI,p_event=pe,
                source_role_id=self.source_role_id,source_bearer=self.source_edge,system_boundary=self.system_boundary,
                source_session_id=(self.source_session_active if self.source_edge in es else None),
                gate_role_id=self.gate_role_id[dim],tune_role_id=self.tune_role_id[dim],chains=chains,
                gate_before=before_gate,gate_after=after_gate,old_gate_exit=handoff,new_gate_acquire=True,
                gate_pass_segment=(self.gate[dim],),gate_bearer_record=dict(role=self.gate_role_id[dim],bearer=self.gate[dim],event_key=ekey),
                switch_instance_id=('IGS',ekey),igs_gamma_frozen=True,seq_domain_frozen=True,sequence_id=self.run_id,org_id=self.anchor)
        self.events.append(ev); return ev

    def external_gate_event(self, dim, p=1.0):
        """Real external gate relocation without the intent source segment sI->a1."""
        preI=self.qualification(); preP=self.P.snapshot(); route=tuple(self.P.execution_path(dim))+(self.gate[dim],)
        self.P._bind_decay_step(); real_route,_=self.F.pulse('a1',p,path=self._admissible_path(route)); es=set(zip(real_route,real_route[1:]))
        for d,D in self.P.dims.items():
            if set(D['R'])<=es: self.P._bind_sediment(d,p)
        self.seq+=1; before_gate=self.gate_state; after_gate=self.gate[dim]; handoff=(before_gate is not None and before_gate!=after_gate); self.gate_state=after_gate
        pe=dict(run_id=self.P.run_id,branch_id=self.P.branch_id,protocol=self.P.protocol,t_run=self.F.t_run,
                kind='external',dim=dim,route=tuple(real_route),pre=preP,post=self.P.snapshot())
        ekey=(self.run_id,self.seq)
        ev=dict(event_id=self.seq,evidence_key=ekey,event_key=ekey,instance_id=('IAct',ekey),gamma_frozen=True,
                run_id=self.run_id,branch_id=self.branch_id,protocol=self.protocol,anchor=self.anchor,execution_class='current',
                kind='external-gate',dim=dim,formal_direction=dim,route=tuple(real_route),preI=preI,p_event=pe,
                source_role_id=self.source_role_id,source_bearer=None,system_boundary=self.system_boundary,
                gate_role_id=self.gate_role_id[dim],tune_role_id=self.tune_role_id[dim],chains=None,
                gate_before=before_gate,gate_after=after_gate,old_gate_exit=handoff,new_gate_acquire=True,
                gate_pass_segment=(self.gate[dim],),gate_bearer_record=dict(role=self.gate_role_id[dim],bearer=self.gate[dim],event_key=ekey),
                switch_instance_id=('IGS',ekey),igs_gamma_frozen=True,seq_domain_frozen=True,sequence_id=self.run_id,org_id=self.anchor)
        self.events.append(ev); return ev

    def _choose_dim(self):
        rel=dict(self.P.Rel()); choices=[d for d,v in rel.items() if v>0]
        r=self.rng.random()*sum(rel[d] for d in choices); chosen=choices[-1]
        for d in choices:
            r-=rel[d]
            if r<=0: chosen=d; break
        return chosen
    def _run_full(self,kind='org',force_dim=None,p=1.0):
        preI=self.qualification(); preP=self.P.snapshot()
        dim=force_dim if force_dim is not None else self._choose_dim()
        route=('sI',)+tuple(self.P.execution_path(dim))+(self.gate[dim],self.tune[dim][1])
        self.P._bind_decay_step()
        real_route,_=self.F.pulse('sI',p,path=self._admissible_path(route))
        es=set(zip(real_route,real_route[1:]))
        for d,D in self.P.dims.items():
            if set(D['R'])<=es: self.P._bind_sediment(d,p)
        self.seq+=1
        before_gate=self.gate_state; after_gate=self.gate[dim]
        handoff=(before_gate is not None and before_gate!=after_gate)
        self.gate_state=after_gate
        pe=dict(run_id=self.P.run_id,branch_id=self.P.branch_id,protocol=self.P.protocol,t_run=self.F.t_run,
                kind=('org' if kind=='org' else 'external'),dim=dim,route=tuple(real_route),pre=preP,post=self.P.snapshot())
        ekey=(self.run_id,self.seq)
        chains=self._source_chains(dim,real_route,pe)
        ev=dict(event_id=self.seq,evidence_key=ekey,event_key=ekey,instance_id=('IAct',ekey),gamma_frozen=True,
                run_id=self.run_id,branch_id=self.branch_id,protocol=self.protocol,anchor=self.anchor,
                execution_class='current',kind=kind,dim=dim,formal_direction=dim,route=tuple(real_route),preI=preI,
                p_event=pe,source_role_id=self.source_role_id,source_bearer=self.source_edge,system_boundary=self.system_boundary,
                source_session_id=(self.source_session_active if self.source_edge in es else None),
                gate_role_id=self.gate_role_id[dim],tune_role_id=self.tune_role_id[dim],chains=chains,
                gate_before=before_gate,gate_after=after_gate,old_gate_exit=handoff,new_gate_acquire=True,
                gate_pass_segment=(self.gate[dim],),gate_bearer_record=dict(role=self.gate_role_id[dim],bearer=self.gate[dim],event_key=ekey),
                switch_instance_id=('IGS',ekey),igs_gamma_frozen=True,seq_domain_frozen=True,sequence_id=self.run_id,org_id=self.anchor)
        self.events.append(ev); return ev
    def org_event(self,p=1.0): return self._run_full('org',None,p)
    def external_event(self,dim,p=1.0): return self._run_full('external',dim,p)
    def source_only_event(self,p=1.0):
        pre=self.qualification(); self.seq+=1
        route,_=self.F.pulse('sI',p,path=self._admissible_path(['sI','a1']))
        es=set(zip(route,route[1:]))
        ekey=(self.run_id,self.seq)
        chains=dict(
            Z_src=dict(role=self.source_role_id,bearer=self.source_edge,segment=tuple(route),actual=(self.source_edge in es),
                       domain_enumerated=True if self.source_edge not in es else False,key=ekey),
            Z_rho=dict(role='ProbabilityGeneration',bearer=None,segment=None,actual=False,domain_enumerated=True,key=ekey),
            Z_G=dict(role='GatePass',bearer=None,segment=None,actual=False,domain_enumerated=True,key=ekey),
            Z_T=dict(role='TuneEntry',bearer=None,segment=None,actual=False,domain_enumerated=True,key=ekey))
        ev=dict(event_id=self.seq,evidence_key=ekey,event_key=ekey,instance_id=('Src',ekey),gamma_frozen=True,
                run_id=self.run_id,branch_id=self.branch_id,protocol=self.protocol,anchor=self.anchor,
                execution_class='current',kind='source-only',route=tuple(route),preI=pre,chains=chains,
                source_role_id=self.source_role_id,source_bearer=self.source_edge,system_boundary=self.system_boundary,
                source_session_id=(self.source_session_active if self.source_edge in es else None),
                sequence_id=self.run_id,org_id=self.anchor)
        self.events.append(ev); return ev
    def A_read(self,ev):
        es=set(zip(ev['route'],ev['route'][1:]))
        A1=(self.source_edge in es and ev['run_id']==self.run_id and ev['anchor']==self.anchor and
            ev.get('source_role_id')==self.source_role_id and ev.get('source_bearer')==self.source_edge)
        pe=ev.get('p_event')
        occ=self.CurrentProbabilityOccurrenceM(pe)
        if occ is True:
            duty=self.ProbabilityDutyConformanceM(pe)
            A2=duty
        elif occ is False:
            A2=False
        else:
            A2=None
        dim=ev.get('dim')
        corr=(dim is not None and self.CorrReadM(dim,ev.get('gate_role_id')) is True)
        A3=(bool(dim in self.gate_link and self.gate_link[dim] in es and corr) if dim else False)
        qok=False
        if dim in self.tune_role_id:
            gr=self.gate_role_id[dim]
            qok=any(q.get('role_id')==ev.get('tune_role_id') and self.PostGateTuneRoleReadM(gr,q) is True
                    for q in self.tune_registry.get(gr,()))
        A4=(bool(dim in self.tune and self.tune[dim] in es and qok) if dim else False)
        vals=[A1,A2,A3,A4]
        bind=(all(v is True for v in vals) and ev.get('evidence_key') is not None)
        read=1 if bind else (0 if any(v is False for v in vals) else None)
        return dict(A1=A1,A2=A2,A3=A3,A4=A4,CurrentProbabilityOccurrence=occ,
                    ProbabilityDutyConformance=(self.ProbabilityDutyConformanceM(pe) if occ is True else None),
                    SameIActBind=bind,Read=read)
    def switch_read(self,prev,cur):
        ap=self.A_read(prev); ac=self.A_read(cur)
        S1=ap['A3']
        S2=ac['A1'] and ac['A2'] and ac['A3']
        S3=bool(prev.get('gate_after') and cur.get('gate_after') and prev['gate_after']!=cur['gate_after'])
        S4=bool(cur.get('old_gate_exit') and cur.get('new_gate_acquire') and cur.get('gate_before')==prev.get('gate_after'))
        Bsame=(prev['run_id']==cur['run_id']==self.run_id and prev['anchor']==cur['anchor']==self.anchor and prev['event_id']<cur['event_id'])
        return dict(S1=S1,S2=S2,S3=S3,S4=S4,B_same=Bsame,Read=1 if all([S1,S2,S3,S4,Bsame]) else 0)


def build(seed=0,formation=80):
    F=m.FLDB(m.NODES+m.EXTRA_NODES+I_NODES,m.MED+m.EXTRA_EDGES+I_EDGES,seed=seed,ld_enabled=True,coupling_enabled=True,zscore=.30)
    m._set_loop(F,False)
    P=pr.ProbabilityOnFLDB(F,seed=seed)
    # Real formation of the two formal probability dimensions and probability binding sediment.
    for _ in range(formation):
        P.formation_pulse('A'); P.formation_pulse('X')
    # Real sedimentation of source and tune interfaces while refreshing both formal Law carriers
    # inside the same W-window; otherwise the legal formation of intent scaffolding would let
    # the upstream A/X Law witnesses decay out of qualification.
    for _ in range(20):
        P.formation_pulse('A'); P.formation_pulse('X')
        F.pulse('sI',1.0,path=['sI','a1'])
        F.pulse('c',1.0,path=['c','gA'])
        F.pulse('x',1.0,path=['x','gX'])
        F.pulse('gA',1.0,path=['gA','tA'])
        F.pulse('gX',1.0,path=['gX','tX'])
    m._set_loop(F,True)
    return IntentOnFLDB(P,seed)

def run_tests(seeds=range(3)):
    fail=[]; checks=0
    def run_seed(seed):
        nonlocal checks
        I=build(seed)
        def ck(name,cond,detail=None):
            nonlocal checks
            checks+=1
            if not cond: fail.append((seed,name,detail))
        q=I.qualification(); ck('qualification',q['Intent'] is True,q)
        L=build(seed+90000); L.intent_loc_declared=None; lq=L.qualification(); ck('intent_loc_open_preserves_tri',lq['IntentLoc'] is None and lq['P_I'] is None and lq['Intent'] is None,lq)
        ck('source_drive_full_semantics',q['SourceDetail']['SourceInternal'] is True and q['SourceDetail']['SourceOrgBind'] is True and q['SourceDetail']['SourceOrgBear'] is True,q['SourceDetail'])
        ck('gate_gc1_gc5',all(q['GateDetail'][f'GC{i}'] is True for i in range(1,6)),q['GateDetail'])
        ck('tune_per_gate',all(v is True for v in q['TuneDetail']['per_gate'].values()),q['TuneDetail'])
        # Source qualification survives current silence: it is organization-level bearing, not a pulse flag.
        ck('source_drive_not_current_pulse',I.SourceDriveM()['Read'] is True,I.SourceDriveM())
        e=I.org_event(); ar=I.A_read(e); ck('action_full',ar['Read']==1,ar)
        G0=build(seed+5); ge=G0.org_gate_event(force_dim='A'); zt=ge['chains']['Z_T']; gcond=G0.ia_conditions(ge)
        ck('gate_stop_no_fabricated_ZT', zt.get('actual') is False and zt.get('segment') is None and
           zt.get('ActualTuneEntrySeg')==0 and zt.get('domain_enumerated') is True and
           G0.tune['A'] not in set(zip(ge['route'],ge['route'][1:])) and G0.tune['A'][1] not in ge['route'] and
           gcond['IA-M5'] and gcond['IA-M10'] and gcond['IA-M11'] and G0.A_read3(ge).get('Read')==0,
           dict(route=ge['route'],Z_T=zt,conditions=gcond,read=G0.A_read3(ge)))
        ex=I.external_event('A'); ax=I.A_read(ex); ck('external_not_full_action',ax['A1'] and ax['CurrentProbabilityOccurrence'] is False and ax['A2'] is False and ax['Read']==0,ax)
        # switch induced through probability generation weighting, not by writing the gate.
        I.P.do_gen_gate('A',0.0); I.P.do_gen_gate('X',1.0); e1=I.org_event()
        I.P.do_gen_gate('A',1.0); I.P.do_gen_gate('X',0.0); e2=I.org_event()
        sw=I.switch_read(e1,e2); ck('gate_switch',sw['Read']==1,(e1['gate_after'],e2['gate_after'],sw))

        # Qualification is not reducible to one sedimented edge: each sealed component can fail independently.
        SD=build(seed+10); SD.source_registry['external_complete_selector']=True
        sd=SD.SourceDriveM(); ck('source_domain_external_selector_fails',sd['SrcDomainBind'] is False and sd['Read'] is False,sd)
        GC=build(seed+20); GC.corr_registry=frozenset(set(GC.corr_registry)|{('A','GateRole:X')})
        gc=GC.GateCorrespondenceM(); ck('gate_nonfunctional_corr_fails',gc['GC2'] is False and gc['Read'] is False,gc)
        GT=build(seed+30); GT.corr_registry=frozenset({('A','GateRole:A')})
        gt=GT.GateCorrespondenceM(); ck('gate_missing_gate_coverage_fails',gt['GC4'] is False and gt['Read'] is False,gt)
        TI=build(seed+40); TI.tune_registry['GateRole:A']=({'role_id':'EffectRole:A','bearer_edge':('gA','tA'),'kind':'effect','anchor':TI.anchor},)
        ti=TI.TuneInterfaceM(); ck('effect_output_not_tune',ti['per_gate']['GateRole:A'] is False and ti['Read'] is False,ti)

        # Source-only world on a fresh object: small readable Degree window (< maxlen), Freeze, then source pulse only.
        S=build(seed+100)
        S.F.degree_trace.clear(); S.F.Freeze()
        S.F.pulse('a1',path=['a1','m1','c']); S.F.pulse('a1',path=['a1','m2','x'])
        d0=S.F.DegreeA(); p0=S.P.snapshot(); se=S.source_only_event(); d1=S.F.DegreeA(); p1=S.P.snapshot()
        pd=S.P.dyn_compare(p0,p1,False,False)
        ck('source_only',se['route']==('sI','a1') and m.cmp_degree(d0,d1)=='Equal' and not pd['RateDyn'] and not pd['DimStructDyn'] and not pd['BindStructDyn'] and not pd['RateBearingStructDyn'],(d0,d1,pd))

        # Carrier-only world: full degree window, Freeze, unrelated field event ages the flow window; probability relation stays exactly fixed.
        C=build(seed+200); C.F.degree_trace.clear(); C.F.Freeze()
        for _ in range(6):
            C.F.pulse('a1',path=['a1','m1','c']); C.F.pulse('a1',path=['a1','m2','x'])
        c0=C.P.snapshot(); d0=C.F.DegreeA(); C.F.pulse('u',path=['u','y','w']); c1=C.P.snapshot(); d1=C.F.DegreeA()
        cpd=C.P.dyn_compare(c0,c1,False,False)
        ck('carrier_only',m.cmp_degree(d0,d1)=='Fall' and cpd['SameRel'] and not cpd['RateDyn'] and not cpd['DimStructDyn'] and not cpd['BindStructDyn'] and not cpd['RateBearingStructDyn'],(d0,d1,cpd))

        # Probability-only world: legal probability run-wiring change, no field/source event, no carrier Degree change.
        Ponly=build(seed+300); d0=Ponly.F.DegreeA(); s0=Ponly.P.snapshot(); n_events=len(Ponly.events)
        op=Ponly.P.do_gen_gate('A',0.2); s1=Ponly.P.snapshot(); d1=Ponly.F.DegreeA(); ppd=Ponly.P.dyn_compare(s0,s1,False,False)
        ck('prob_only',ppd['RateDyn'] and m.cmp_degree(d0,d1)=='Equal' and len(Ponly.events)==n_events and Ponly.P.audit_authoritative_ops(),(s0['Rel'],s1['Rel'],d0,d1,ppd,op))
    for s in seeds: run_seed(s)
    return dict(seeds=len(list(seeds)),checks=checks,failures=fail,TOTAL_PASS=not fail)


# ===== Consolidated IDyn/PDyn runtime-certificate layer (v13) =====
OPEN=None
IA_SRC_KEYS=('IA-M1','IA-M2','IA-M3','IA-M4','IA-M5','IA-M11','IA-M12')
IA_ALL_KEYS=tuple(f'IA-M{i}' for i in range(1,13))
PD_KEYS=tuple(f'PD-M{i}' for i in range(1,14))

def _event_actual(I,e):
    return isinstance(e,dict) and e in I.events and e.get('run_id')==I.run_id and e.get('branch_id')==I.branch_id and e.get('execution_class')=='current'

def _flog_matches(I,e):
    if not I.F.log or not isinstance(e,dict): return False
    t,entry,route,_=I.F.log[-1]
    return tuple(route)==tuple(e.get('route',())) and t==I.F.t_run

def source_window_solver(I, events, span=None):
    """Derive source/action process classes from closed event units.

    SourceStart/SourceStop controller operations are retained only as input/session commands;
    they are not used as SrcStart^M/SrcStop^M truth labels.  Cross-time classes are derived
    from SrcOcc / NoSrc and A / NoAct over the pre-frozen event-unit grain delta=1.
    """
    events=list(events)
    units=[]; all_src=True; all_act=True
    for idx,e in enumerate(events):
        ia=I.ia_conditions(e)
        src_ok=all(ia.get(k) is True for k in IA_SRC_KEYS)
        act_ok=all(ia.get(k) is True for k in IA_ALL_KEYS)
        actual=_event_actual(I,e)
        edges=set(zip(e.get('route',()),e.get('route',())[1:]))
        has_source=I.source_edge in edges
        src_occ=bool(actual and has_source and src_ok)
        # Negative source coverage does not require a positive A1 witness.  A current event whose
        # complete actual route is recorded and excludes the frozen source edge is a positive NoSrc unit.
        no_src=bool(actual and not has_source)
        src_unit_closed=bool(src_occ or no_src)
        ar=I.A_read3(e)
        act_occ=bool(actual and act_ok and ar.get('Read')==1)
        no_act=bool(actual and act_ok and ar.get('Read')==0)
        units.append(dict(index=idx,event_id=e.get('event_id'),evidence_key=e.get('evidence_key'),
                          IA=ia,src_IA_ok=src_ok,act_IA_ok=act_ok,SrcOcc=src_occ,NoSrc=no_src,
                          ActOcc=act_occ,NoAct=no_act,A=ar))
        all_src &= src_unit_closed; all_act &= act_ok

    def classify(pos_key,neg_key):
        # Maximal positive intervals at frozen unit grain; Stop needs a following covered
        # negative unit. Start requires a preceding in-segment covered NoX unit; when the
        # positive interval touches the declared left boundary, Start is out of domain.
        intervals=[]; i=0
        while i < len(units):
            if not units[i][pos_key]: i+=1; continue
            j=i
            while j+1<len(units) and units[j+1][pos_key]: j+=1
            intervals.append((i,j)); i=j+1
        starts=[]; continues=[]; stops=[]
        for i,j in intervals:
            # A positive interval touching the declared left boundary has no preceding
            # in-segment negative-coverage window.  Start is therefore out of this
            # declaration's evaluable domain, not TRUE.
            if i>0 and units[i-1][neg_key]: starts.append(i)
            if j>i: continues.extend(range(i+1,j+1))
            if j+1<len(units) and units[j+1][neg_key]: stops.append(j+1)
        # Restart (sealed IDyn definition 15) additionally requires the whole-segment qualification
        # Read_{IntentQualWholeSegment}=1, read from the same RuntimeAudit span.  A Stop->Start
        # candidate without that span certificate, or with L4 != 1, is OPEN, never TRUE.
        candidate=any(st>sp for sp in stops for st in starts)
        closed=all(u[pos_key] or u[neg_key] for u in units)
        if candidate:
            restart=True if (span is not None and span.get('readable') and span.get('L4')==1) else None
        else:
            restart=False if closed else None
        return dict(intervals=tuple(intervals),Start=tuple(starts),Continue=tuple(continues),Stop=tuple(stops),Restart=restart)

    src_proc=classify('SrcOcc','NoSrc')
    act_proc=classify('ActOcc','NoAct')
    # Solvability: every declared unit has the needed direct representations and the grain is frozen.
    delta_src=(I.idyn_protocol.get('delta_src')==1 and I.idyn_protocol.get('status')=='frozen')
    delta_act=(I.idyn_protocol.get('delta_act')==1 and I.idyn_protocol.get('status')=='frozen')
    event_span_ok=True if span is None else all(event_covered_by_span(e,span) for e in events)
    return dict(M8=bool(events and all_src and delta_src and event_span_ok),
                M12=bool(events and all_act and delta_act and event_span_ok),
                units=units,delta_src=delta_src,delta_act=delta_act,
                SourceProcess=src_proc,ActionProcess=act_proc,
                # commands are reported only as auxiliary runtime records
                source_commands=tuple(copy.deepcopy(I.source_session_history)))

def carrier_solver(I, endpoints):
    """M9/M10 backing solver: frozen candidate registry, pointwise BearDim, same-carrier degree domain."""
    reg=tuple(I.P.candidate_registry_frozen)
    expected=tuple(sorted((k,v['dim'],v['role'],v['path']) for k,v in I.P.carrier_candidates.items()))
    frozen=(reg==expected)
    points=[]
    for ep in endpoints:
        # endpoint contains a deep-copied world, so BearDim is read at that point rather than from current state.
        J=ep['world']
        bears={cid:J.P.BearDimM(cid,C['dim']) for cid,C in J.P.carrier_candidates.items()}
        points.append(dict(bear=bears,degreeA=J.F.DegreeA(),registry=J.P.candidate_registry_frozen))
    pointwise=bool(points and all(set(p['bear'])==set(I.P.carrier_candidates) for p in points))
    common=[]
    if len(points)>=2:
        for cid in I.P.carrier_candidates:
            if points[0]['bear'][cid] and points[1]['bear'][cid]: common.append(cid)
    # reference spec has DegreeA only for primary A role; M10 is readable when that same carrier bears both endpoints.
    degree_readable=('A_primary' in common and points[0]['degreeA'] is not None and points[1]['degreeA'] is not None)
    step=(m.cmp_degree(points[0]['degreeA'],points[1]['degreeA']) if degree_readable else 'NotApplicable') if len(points)>=2 else None
    return dict(M9=bool(frozen and pointwise),M10=bool(degree_readable),registry=reg,points=points,common_bearers=tuple(common),DegreeStep=step)



class RuntimeAuditV14:
    """Single certificate source for LegalSeg / IDyn-M3-M4 / PDyn-M3 in Spec_IB^ref R&D.
    Every admitted top-level state operation records one full-state digest transition plus the
    post-operation qualification. Event-producing calls additionally record the returned actual
    event identity/evidence key. The ledger is passive and never updates field/probability state.
    """
    LEGAL={
      'SourceStart','SourceStop','GenGate','CarrierLink','IntentAvail','RunAvail',
      'OrgEvent','OrgGateEvent','ExternalEvent','ExternalGateEvent','SourceOnlyEvent',
      'Freeze','Thaw','IdleStep','Pulse'
    }
    EVENT_OPS={'OrgEvent','OrgGateEvent','ExternalEvent','ExternalGateEvent','SourceOnlyEvent'}
    def __init__(self,I):
        self.I=I; self.entries=[]; self.seq=0
        d=self.state_digest(); self.entries.append(self._rec('Genesis',d,d,None))
    def state_digest(self):
        I=self.I; F=I.F; P=I.P
        st=(F.t_run,F.t_sed,F.SED,
            tuple((n,F.d[n]) for n in F.nodes),
            tuple((e,F.g[e],tuple(F.rep[e]),F.Om[e],F.th[e],F.Avail[e],F.mask[e]) for e in F.E),
            tuple(sorted((k,tuple(v)) for k,v in F.pathdyn.items())),tuple(F.degree_trace),len(F.log),repr(F.rng.getstate()),
            tuple(sorted(P.bind.items())),tuple(sorted((k,tuple(v)) for k,v in P.bind_rep.items())),
            tuple(sorted(P.gen_gate.items())),tuple(sorted(getattr(P,'carrier_link',{}).items())),
            len(P.events),len(P.authoritative_ops),repr(P.rng.getstate()),
            I.gate_state,I.source_session_active,I.seq,len(I.events),len(I.intent_ops),repr(I.rng.getstate()))
        return hashlib.sha256(repr(st).encode()).hexdigest()
    def _identity_snapshot(self):
        I=self.I
        return dict(anchor=I.anchor,run_id=I.run_id,branch_id=I.branch_id,
                    prob_run_id=I.P.run_id,prob_branch_id=I.P.branch_id,
                    source_role=I.source_role_id,source_registry=copy.deepcopy(I.source_registry),
                    gate_roles=copy.deepcopy(I.gate_role_id),gate_bearers=copy.deepcopy(I.gate_role_bearers),
                    corr_registry=tuple(sorted(I.corr_registry)),tune_roles=copy.deepcopy(I.tune_role_id),
                    tune_registry=copy.deepcopy(I.tune_registry),candidate_registry=tuple(I.P.candidate_registry_frozen))
    def _protocol_snapshot(self):
        I=self.I
        return dict(intent_protocol=I.protocol,prob_protocol=I.P.protocol,
                    intent_history=copy.deepcopy(I.protocol_history),prob_history=copy.deepcopy(I.P.protocol_history),
                    idyn=copy.deepcopy(I.idyn_protocol),idyn_history=copy.deepcopy(I.idyn_protocol_history))
~~~
