~~~python
import importlib.util,sys,subprocess,json,copy,hashlib,pathlib
from pathlib import Path
HERE=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
PATH=str(HERE/'intent_on_fldb_rnd_v14.py')
spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('ir7',PATH); ir=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec); sys.modules[spec.name]=ir; spec.loader.exec_module(ir)
m=ir.m
OPEN=None

def action_state(I,e):
    r=I.A_read(e)
    return dict(full=bool(r['Read']==1),A1=r['A1'],A2=r['A2'],A3=r['A3'],A4=r['A4'])

def direction_step(p0,p1,dim='A',cmpdim='X'):
    r0=dict(p0['Rel']); r1=dict(p1['Rel'])
    if dim not in p0['V'] or dim not in p1['V'] or cmpdim not in p0['V'] or cmpdim not in p1['V']:
        return 'NotApplicable'
    a0,b0=r0.get(dim,0.0),r0.get(cmpdim,0.0); a1,b1=r1.get(dim,0.0),r1.get(cmpdim,0.0)
    if a0+b0<=0 or a1+b1<=0: return 'NotApplicable'
    s0=a0/(a0+b0); s1=a1/(a1+b1)
    if abs(s1-s0)<=1e-12: return 'Equal'
    return 'Rise' if s1>s0 else 'Fall'

def profile_result(dir_step,car_step):
    if dir_step=='NotApplicable' or car_step=='NotApplicable': return 'ProfileNotApplicable'
    if dir_step is None or car_step is None: return OPEN
    ups={'Rise'}; downs={'Fall'}
    if dir_step in ups and car_step in downs: return 'Incomparable'
    if dir_step in downs and car_step in ups: return 'Incomparable'
    if dir_step=='Migration' or car_step=='Migration': return 'Incomparable'
    vals={dir_step,car_step}
    if vals<={'Rise','Equal'} and 'Rise' in vals: return 'Rise'
    if vals<={'Fall','Equal'} and 'Fall' in vals: return 'Fall'
    if vals=={'Equal'}: return 'Equal'
    return OPEN

def prob_bind_reader(target_I, evidence, correct_evidence_available):
    # Foreign evidence is discarded before object reading. If no correct target evidence remains,
    # the target probability-dynamics read stays OPEN, never 0.
    same=(evidence.get('run_id')==target_I.P.run_id and evidence.get('protocol')==target_I.P.protocol)
    if not same:
        return dict(Read=(0 if False else OPEN),reason=('ProbBindMismatch' if not correct_evidence_available else 'RetryCorrectObject'))
    return dict(Read=evidence.get('Read'),reason=None)

def model_digest(I):
    F=I.F
    return (F.dyn_snapshot(),tuple(sorted(I.P.bind.items())),tuple(sorted(I.P.gen_gate.items())),
            tuple(I.events),tuple(I.intent_ops),I.gate_state,I.source_session_active)

def run(seeds=range(5)):
    failures=[]; checks=0; observations=[]
    def ck(seed,name,cond,detail=None):
        nonlocal checks; checks+=1
        if not cond: failures.append((seed,name,detail))

    for seed in seeds:
        # W1: source has an explicit ongoing source session before complete action first starts.
        I=ir.build(seed)
        I.P.do_gen_gate('A',1.0); I.P.do_gen_gate('X',0.0)
        sid=I.source_start(); e0=I.source_only_event(); e1=I.source_only_event(); e2=I.org_event()
        a0,a1,a2=action_state(I,e0),action_state(I,e1),action_state(I,e2)
        proc=ir.source_window_solver(I,[e0,e1,e2])
        # A positive interval that begins at the declared left boundary is not a formal Start:
        # there is no in-gamma preceding NoSrc/NoAct coverage for [tau-delta,tau].
        ck(seed,'W0_left_boundary_positive_not_Start',
           proc['SourceProcess']['Start']==() and proc['ActionProcess']['Start']==(), proc)
        ck(seed,'W1_source_cont_before_first_full_action',
           proc['SourceProcess']['intervals']==((0,2),) and 1 in proc['SourceProcess']['Continue'] and
           proc['ActionProcess']['Start']==() and a0['A1'] and a1['A1'] and (not a0['full']) and (not a1['full']) and a2['full'],
           (proc,sid,e0,e1,e2,a0,a1,a2))

        # W2a: full action stops at gate layer while ProbabilityAct continues, then gate recovery restarts full action.
        # All operations run through the sole RuntimeAuditV14; Restart reads L4 of the same span.
        G=ir.build(seed+100); AG=ir.RuntimeAuditV14(G); AG.gen_gate('A',1.0); AG.gen_gate('X',0.0); AG.source_start(); gs0=AG.point()
        g0=AG.org_event(); q0=G.qualification();
        AG.intent_avail(G.gate_link['A'],0); g1=AG.org_event(); q1=G.qualification();
        AG.intent_avail(G.gate_link['A'],1); g2=AG.org_event(); q2=G.qualification(); gs1=AG.point(); gspan=AG.span(gs0,gs1)
        ag0,ag1,ag2=action_state(G,g0),action_state(G,g1),action_state(G,g2)
        pg=[G.P.ProbabilityAct(x['p_event']) for x in (g0,g1,g2)]
        gp=ir.source_window_solver(G,[g0,g1,g2],gspan)
        # Removing the negative-coverage material from the middle unit must remove the Stop classification.
        g1open=copy.deepcopy(g1); g1open['chains']=None
        gp_open=ir.source_window_solver(G,[g0,g1open])
        ck(seed,'W2_gate_recovery_restart_with_prob_continuing',
           all(pg) and q0['Intent'] and q1['Intent'] and q2['Intent'] and ag0['full'] and (not ag1['full']) and
           ag1['A2'] and not ag1['A3'] and ag2['full'] and gspan['L4']==1 and
           gp['ActionProcess']['Stop']==(1,) and gp['ActionProcess']['Restart'] is True and
           gp_open['ActionProcess']['Stop']==(),
           (pg,gp['ActionProcess'],gp_open['ActionProcess'],ag0,ag1,ag2,q0,q1,q2))

        # W2b: same, but stop/restart is at tune layer.
        T=ir.build(seed+200); AT=ir.RuntimeAuditV14(T); AT.gen_gate('A',1.0); AT.gen_gate('X',0.0); AT.source_start(); ts0=AT.point()
        t0=AT.org_event(); tq0=T.qualification(); AT.intent_avail(T.tune['A'],0); t1=AT.org_event(); tq1=T.qualification();
        AT.intent_avail(T.tune['A'],1); t2=AT.org_event(); tq2=T.qualification(); ts1=AT.point(); tspan=AT.span(ts0,ts1)
        at0,at1,at2=action_state(T,t0),action_state(T,t1),action_state(T,t2)
        pt=[T.P.ProbabilityAct(x['p_event']) for x in (t0,t1,t2)]
        tp=ir.source_window_solver(T,[t0,t1,t2],tspan)
        ck(seed,'W3_tune_recovery_restart_with_prob_continuing',
           all(pt) and tq0['Intent'] and tq1['Intent'] and tq2['Intent'] and at0['full'] and (not at1['full']) and
           at1['A2'] and at1['A3'] and not at1['A4'] and at2['full'] and tspan['L4']==1 and
           tp['ActionProcess']['Stop']==(1,) and tp['ActionProcess']['Restart'] is True,
           (pt,tp['ActionProcess'],at0,at1,at2,tq0,tq1,tq2))

        # W4: SourceStop/SourceStart commands do not themselves prove cross-time type.
        # A covered NoSrc unit between two positive source intervals does.
        S=ir.build(seed+300); sid1=S.source_start(); s0=S.source_only_event(); S.source_stop(); sid2=S.source_start(); s1cmd=S.source_only_event()
        command_only=ir.source_window_solver(S,[s0,s1cmd])
        # The commands alone do not classify Stop/Restart.  Add a covered NoSrc event and then a new source occurrence.
        S2=ir.build(seed+301); AS2=ir.RuntimeAuditV14(S2); ss0=AS2.point(); sid1b=AS2.source_start(); s0b=AS2.source_only_event(); AS2.source_stop()
        quiet=AS2.external_gate_event('A'); sid2b=AS2.source_start(); s1=AS2.source_only_event(); ss1=AS2.point(); sspan=AS2.span(ss0,ss1)
        sp=ir.source_window_solver(S2,[s0b,quiet,s1],sspan)
        ck(seed,'W4_source_stop_restart_derived_from_occurrence_and_NoSrc',
           command_only['SourceProcess']['Stop']==() and command_only['SourceProcess']['Restart'] is False and
           sp['SourceProcess']['intervals']==((0,0),(2,2)) and sp['SourceProcess']['Stop']==(1,) and
           sp['SourceProcess']['Restart'] is True and sspan['L4']==1 and sid1b!=sid2b and quiet['source_bearer'] is None and S2.qualification()['Intent'] is True,
           (command_only['SourceProcess'],sp['SourceProcess'],sid1,sid2,sid1b,sid2b,S2.source_session_history))

        # W4b: the same SrcOcc-NoSrc-SrcOcc sequence without the span certificate, and with a span whose
        # qualification exits and recovers inside, cannot sign Restart (definition 15 needs L4=1).
        sp_nospan=ir.source_window_solver(S2,[s0b,quiet,s1])
        R3=ir.build(seed+302); AR3=ir.RuntimeAuditV14(R3); rr0=AR3.point(); r_a=AR3.source_only_event(); r_q=AR3.external_gate_event('A')
        AR3.gen_gate('A',0.0); AR3.gen_gate('X',0.0); AR3.gen_gate('A',1.0); AR3.gen_gate('X',1.0)
        r_b=AR3.source_only_event(); rr1=AR3.point(); rspan=AR3.span(rr0,rr1)
        rp=ir.source_window_solver(R3,[r_a,r_q,r_b],rspan)
        ck(seed,'W4b_restart_requires_whole_segment_qualification',
           sp_nospan['SourceProcess']['Stop']==(1,) and sp_nospan['SourceProcess']['Restart'] is None and
           rspan['L4']==0 and rp['SourceProcess']['Stop']==(1,) and rp['SourceProcess']['Restart'] is None,
           (sp_nospan['SourceProcess'],rspan['L4'],rp['SourceProcess']))

        # W4c: whole-segment qualification missing (a deleted record) or undetermined (a point with
        # declared location unknown) also blocks Restart; Stop is still derived from the units.
        M1=ir.build(seed+303); AM1=ir.RuntimeAuditV14(M1); m0=AM1.point(); ma=AM1.source_only_event(); mq=AM1.external_gate_event('A'); mb=AM1.source_only_event(); m1=AM1.point()
        AM1.entries[m0['index']+2]['qual']=None; mspan=AM1.span(m0,m1); mp=ir.source_window_solver(M1,[ma,mq,mb],mspan)
        M2=ir.build(seed+304); AM2=ir.RuntimeAuditV14(M2); n0=AM2.point(); na=AM2.source_only_event(); M2.intent_loc_declared=None; nq=AM2.external_gate_event('A'); M2.intent_loc_declared=True; nb=AM2.source_only_event(); n1=AM2.point()
        nspan=AM2.span(n0,n1); np_=ir.source_window_solver(M2,[na,nq,nb],nspan)
        ck(seed,'W4c_restart_blocked_by_missing_or_open_qualification',
           mspan['L4'] is None and mp['SourceProcess']['Stop']==(1,) and mp['SourceProcess']['Restart'] is None and
           nspan['L4'] is None and np_['SourceProcess']['Stop']==(1,) and np_['SourceProcess']['Restart'] is None,
           (mspan['L4'],mp['SourceProcess'],nspan['L4'],np_['SourceProcess']))

        # W5: foreign probability evidence is real, but cannot be used for this target P_I; without correct target evidence result remains OPEN.
        A=ir.build(seed+400); B=ir.build(seed+401)
        b0=B.P.snapshot(); B.P.do_gen_gate('A',0.2); b1=B.P.snapshot(); bpd=B.P.dyn_compare(b0,b1,False,False)
        foreign=dict(run_id=B.P.run_id,protocol=B.P.protocol,Read=1 if bpd['RateDyn'] else 0,evidence_key=(B.P.run_id,'foreign-rate'))
        br=prob_bind_reader(A,foreign,correct_evidence_available=False)
        ck(seed,'W5_foreign_prob_evidence_does_not_zero_target',bpd['RateDyn'] and br['Read'] is None and br['reason']=='ProbBindMismatch',(foreign,br))

        # W6: current gate switch can be positive while source cross-time type remains unclassified/open without source-session transition material.
        SW=ir.build(seed+500); SW.P.do_gen_gate('A',0.0); SW.P.do_gen_gate('X',1.0); sw0=SW.org_event();
        SW.P.do_gen_gate('A',1.0); SW.P.do_gen_gate('X',0.0); sw1=SW.org_event(); sr=SW.switch_read(sw0,sw1)
        source_transition='SourceTransitionOpen' if not SW.source_session_history else 'classified'
        ck(seed,'W6_switch_current_not_cross_time_type',sr['Read']==1 and source_transition=='SourceTransitionOpen',(sr,SW.source_session_history))

        # W7: direction A rises while carrier A falls in the same declared step -> product profile is Incomparable, no scalar total strength.
        M=ir.build(seed+600); M.F.degree_trace.clear(); M.F.Freeze()
        for _ in range(6):
            M.F.pulse('a1',path=['a1','m1','c']); M.F.pulse('a1',path=['a1','m2','x'])
        p0=M.P.snapshot(); d0=M.F.DegreeA(); M.P.do_gen_gate('A',2.0); M.F.pulse('u',path=['u','y','w']); p1=M.P.snapshot(); d1=M.F.DegreeA()
        ds=direction_step(p0,p1,'A','X'); cs=m.cmp_degree(d0,d1); prof=profile_result(ds,cs)
        ck(seed,'W7_mixed_direction_rise_carrier_fall_incomparable',ds=='Rise' and cs=='Fall' and prof=='Incomparable',(p0['Rel'],p1['Rel'],d0,d1,ds,cs,prof))

        # W8: ProfileNotApplicable without losing qualification.  Train a legal alternate A bearer,
        # preserve the intent scaffolding, then perform make-before-break A bearer replacement.
        # No single A bearer satisfies BearHold across the comparison step, so the required carrier
        # coordinate has no common comparison domain.  This is a determined N/A result, not OPEN/0.
        N=ir.build(seed+700); m._set_loop(N.F,False)
        for _ in range(10):
            N.P.formation_candidate('A_alt'); N.P.formation_pulse('A'); N.P.formation_pulse('X')
            N.F.pulse('sI',1.0,path=['sI','a1']); N.F.pulse('c',1.0,path=['c','gA']); N.F.pulse('x',1.0,path=['x','gX'])
            N.F.pulse('gA',1.0,path=['gA','tA']); N.F.pulse('gX',1.0,path=['gX','tX'])
        m._set_loop(N.F,True)
        qn0=N.qualification(); n0=N.P.snapshot(); b0=set(N.P.current_bearers('A'))
        N.P.do_carrier_link('A_alt',1); N.P.do_carrier_link('A_primary',0)
        qn1=N.qualification(); n1=N.P.snapshot(); b1=set(N.P.current_bearers('A'))
        nd=direction_step(n0,n1,'A','X'); car_step=('NotApplicable' if not (b0 & b1) else 'Equal'); profna=profile_result(nd,car_step)
        ck(seed,'W8_profile_not_applicable_is_determined_result',qn0['Intent'] and qn1['Intent'] and not (b0 & b1) and car_step=='NotApplicable' and profna=='ProfileNotApplicable',(n0['Rel'],n1['Rel'],b0,b1,qn0,qn1,nd,car_step,profna))

        # W9: reader non-interference at process level under identical operation plan.
        R0=ir.build(seed+800); R1=copy.deepcopy(R0)
        # Same source-session and runtime plan; R1 adds readers between operations.
        R0.source_start(); R1.source_start();
        plan=[]
        for k in range(3):
            e0=R0.source_only_event();
            _=R1.qualification(); _=R1.P.snapshot(); _=R1.F.DegreeA(); e1r=R1.source_only_event(); _=R1.A_read(e1r)
            plan.append((e0['route'],e1r['route']))
        R0.source_stop(); R1.source_stop()
        # Ignore passive event object identity strings and compare underlying field/probability state only.
        core0=(R0.F.dyn_snapshot(),tuple(sorted(R0.P.bind.items())),tuple(sorted(R0.P.gen_gate.items())))
        core1=(R1.F.dyn_snapshot(),tuple(sorted(R1.P.bind.items())),tuple(sorted(R1.P.gen_gate.items())))
        ck(seed,'W9_process_reader_noninterference',core0==core1,(plan,core0==core1))

        # W10: legal carrier-bearer replacement under a frozen candidate discovery rule.
        # The alternate A candidate is formed by real sedimentation while not currently wired into L_P.
        # Handoff is make-before-break: enable A_alt role wiring, then disable A_primary.  Qualification
        # must hold throughout; neither candidate has BearHold across the endpoint pair, so replacement
        # is not mis-signed as one carrier's Degree step.  L3 anchor and L6 protocol/discovery stay fixed.
        H=ir.build(seed+900); HP=H.P; HF=H.F
        ir.m._set_loop(HF,False)
        for _ in range(10):
            HP.formation_candidate('A_alt'); HP.formation_pulse('A'); HP.formation_pulse('X')
            HF.pulse('sI',path=['sI','a1']); HF.pulse('c',path=['c','gA']); HF.pulse('x',path=['x','gX'])
            HF.pulse('gA',path=['gA','tA']); HF.pulse('gX',path=['gX','tX'])
        ir.m._set_loop(HF,True)
        reg0=HP.candidate_registry_frozen; protoI=tuple(H.protocol_history); protoP=tuple(HP.protocol_history); anchor0=H.anchor
        laws0={c:HP._candidate_law(c) for c in HP.carrier_candidates}
        hb0={c:HP.BearDimM(c,HP.carrier_candidates[c]['dim']) for c in HP.carrier_candidates}; hs0=HP.snapshot(); hq0=H.qualification()
        hop1=HP.do_carrier_link('A_alt',1); hqmid=H.qualification(); hlpmid=HP.current_LP()
        hop2=HP.do_carrier_link('A_primary',0); hq1=H.qualification(); hs1=HP.snapshot()
        hb1={c:HP.BearDimM(c,HP.carrier_candidates[c]['dim']) for c in HP.carrier_candidates}
        hpd=HP.dyn_compare(hs0,hs1,False,False)
        primary_hold=hb0['A_primary'] and hb1['A_primary']; alt_hold=hb0['A_alt'] and hb1['A_alt']
        L3=(H.anchor==anchor0); L6=(tuple(H.protocol_history)==protoI and tuple(HP.protocol_history)==protoP and HP.candidate_registry_frozen==reg0)
        ck(seed,'W10_carrier_bearer_legal_replacement',
           all(laws0.values()) and hq0['Intent'] and hqmid['Intent'] and hq1['Intent'] and
           hb0['A_primary'] and (not hb0['A_alt']) and (not hb1['A_primary']) and hb1['A_alt'] and
           ('A_primary','A','ProbDir:A') in hs0['DimOrg'] and ('A_alt','A','ProbDir:A') in hs1['DimOrg'] and
           (not primary_hold) and (not alt_hold) and hpd['DimStructDyn'] and L3 and L6 and HP.audit_authoritative_ops(),
           dict(laws=laws0,q=(hq0,hqmid,hq1),bear=(hb0,hb1),lp=(hs0['DimOrg'],hlpmid,hs1['DimOrg']),pd=hpd,L3=L3,L6=L6,ops=(hop1,hop2)))

        observations.append(dict(seed=seed,W1=(sid,a0,a1,a2),W2=(ag0,ag1,ag2),W3=(at0,at1,at2),W4=(sid1,sid2),W5=br,W6=sr,W7=(ds,cs,prof),W8=(nd,profna),W10=dict(L3=L3,L6=L6,bear=(hb0,hb1),pd=hpd)))
    return dict(seeds=len(list(seeds)),checks=checks,failures=failures,TOTAL_PASS=not failures,observations=observations[:1],
                OPEN_GAPS=[])

def _jsonable(x):
    if isinstance(x,dict): return {str(k):_jsonable(v) for k,v in x.items()}
    if isinstance(x,(list,tuple,set)): return [_jsonable(v) for v in x]
    return x

if __name__=='__main__':
    cp=subprocess.run([sys.executable,PATH],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=300)
    ok=(cp.returncode==0 and '[UPSTREAM+PROB_FULL] PASS' in cp.stdout and '"TOTAL_PASS": true' in cp.stdout)
    print('[FULL_INHERITED_MODEL]', 'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL')
    if not ok:
        print(cp.stdout[-12000:]); print(cp.stderr[-4000:]); raise SystemExit(2)
    r=run(range(5)); print(json.dumps(_jsonable(r),indent=2,default=str)); raise SystemExit(0 if r['TOTAL_PASS'] else 1)
~~~

import importlib.util,sys,json,copy,subprocess
from pathlib import Path
HERE=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
IP=str(HERE/'intent_on_fldb_rnd_v14.py')
JP=str(HERE/'intent_judge_matrix_rnd_v14.py')
s=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('ir8s',IP); ir=importlib.util.module_from_spec(s); sys.modules[s.name]=ir; s.loader.exec_module(ir)
j=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('j8s',JP); jm=importlib.util.module_from_spec(j); sys.modules[j.name]=jm; j.loader.exec_module(jm)

def run():
  failures=[]; checks=0; stress=[]; l5=[]
  def ck(name,c,detail=None):
    nonlocal checks; checks+=1
    if not c: failures.append((name,detail))
  # finite local robustness grid over construction/run parameters, not a universal robustness theorem.
  cases=[]
  for seed in range(4):
    for formation in (60,80,100): cases.append((seed,formation))
  for seed,formation in cases:
    I=ir.build(seed,formation=formation); q=I.qualification(); ck(f'qual_{seed}_{formation}',q['Intent'],q)
    # current full action under three positive pulse strengths
    acts=[]
    for p in (0.5,1.0,2.0):
      e=I.org_event(p=p); r=I.A_read(e); acts.append(r['Read']); ck(f'act_{seed}_{formation}_{p}',r['Read']==1,r)
    # reversible probability-side wiring changes preserve qualification while producing direction comparison material.
    rel0=I.P.Rel(); I.P.do_gen_gate('A',0.25); rel1=I.P.Rel(); I.P.do_gen_gate('A',1.0); rel2=I.P.Rel()
    ck(f'gen_gate_qual_{seed}_{formation}',I.qualification()['Intent'],(rel0,rel1,rel2))
    ck(f'gen_gate_reversible_{seed}_{formation}',I.P._same_rel(rel0,rel2) and not I.P._same_rel(rel0,rel1),(rel0,rel1,rel2))
    stress.append((seed,formation,acts,rel0,rel1))

  # L5 probe family.  All L1-L6 values come from the same RuntimeAuditV14 span.
  I=ir.build(777); AI=ir.RuntimeAuditV14(I); base0=AI.point(); ev=AI.source_only_event(); base1=AI.point()
  clean=jm.legal_segment_audit(I,AI,base0,base1,[ev])
  ck('l5_clean',all(clean[k]==1 for k in ('L1','L2','L3','L4','L5','L6')),clean)
  probes=[]
  for tag,mut in [
    ('foreign_run',{'run_id':'foreign'}),
    ('foreign_anchor',{'anchor':'foreign-anchor'}),
    ('foreign_both',{'run_id':'foreign','anchor':'foreign-anchor'}),
    ('foreign_evidence_key',{'evidence_key':('foreign','key')})]:
      evidence=copy.deepcopy(ev); evidence.update(mut)
      a=jm.legal_segment_audit(I,AI,base0,base1,[evidence])
      probes.append((tag,a)); ck('l5_'+tag,a['L5'] is None and a['L5_reason']=='EvidenceSplice' and all(a[k]==1 for k in ('L1','L2','L3','L4','L6')),a)
  # Physical/identity/protocol perturbations hit their own legal-segment layers; they do not create L5=0.
  D=ir.build(779); AD=ir.RuntimeAuditV14(D); d0=AD.point(); D.F.d['m1']+=0.123456789; AD.source_only_event(); d1=AD.point()
  a2=jm.legal_segment_audit(D,AD,d0,d1,[]); probes.append(('direct_write',a2)); ck('l5_search_L2',a2['L2']==0 and a2['L5']==1,a2)
  A=ir.build(780); AA=ir.RuntimeAuditV14(A); a0=AA.point(); A.anchor='other'; AA.source_only_event(); a1=AA.point()
  a3=jm.legal_segment_audit(A,AA,a0,a1,[]); probes.append(('anchor_change',a3)); ck('l5_search_L3',a3['L3']==0 and a3['L5']==1,a3)
  R=ir.build(782); AR=ir.RuntimeAuditV14(R); r0=AR.point(); R.gate_role_id['A']='GateRole:Changed'; AR.source_only_event(); r1=AR.point(); ar3=jm.legal_segment_audit(R,AR,r0,r1,[]); probes.append(('role_identity_change',ar3)); ck('l5_search_L3_role_identity',ar3['L3']==0 and ar3['L5']==1,ar3)
  U=ir.build(781); AU=ir.RuntimeAuditV14(U); u0=AU.point(); U.idyn_protocol['coverage_rule']='posthoc'; AU.source_only_event(); u1=AU.point()
  a6=jm.legal_segment_audit(U,AU,u0,u1,[]); probes.append(('protocol_change',a6)); ck('l5_search_L6',a6['L6']==0 and a6['L5']==1,a6)
  V=ir.build(783); AV=ir.RuntimeAuditV14(V); v0=AV.point(); V.idyn_protocol['delta_act']=2; AV.source_only_event(); v1=AV.point(); av6=jm.legal_segment_audit(V,AV,v0,v1,[]); probes.append(('grain_change',av6)); ck('l5_search_L6_grain',av6['L6']==0 and av6['L5']==1,av6)
  Q=ir.build(778); AQ=ir.RuntimeAuditV14(Q); qst=AQ.point(); AQ.gen_gate('A',0.0); AQ.gen_gate('X',0.0); AQ.gen_gate('A',1.0); AQ.gen_gate('X',1.0); qen=AQ.point()
  aq=jm.legal_segment_audit(Q,AQ,qst,qen,[]); probes.append(('qual_exit',aq)); ck('l5_search_L4',aq['L4']==0 and aq['L5']==1,aq)
  # No probe is permitted to return an object-level L5=0 in current sealed representation.
  independent=[(tag,a) for tag,a in probes if a.get('L5')==0 and all(a.get(k)==1 for k in ('L1','L2','L3','L4','L6'))]
  ck('l5_no_independent_object_failure_found',len(independent)==0,independent)
  return dict(stress_cases=len(cases),checks=checks,failures=failures,TOTAL_PASS=not failures,
              L5_independent_object_failures_found=len(independent),stress_sample=stress[:2],L5_probes=probes)

def _jsonable(x):
  if isinstance(x,dict): return {str(k):_jsonable(v) for k,v in x.items()}
  if isinstance(x,(list,tuple,set)): return [_jsonable(v) for v in x]
  return x

if __name__=='__main__':
  cp=subprocess.run([sys.executable,IP],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=300)
  ok=cp.returncode==0 and '[UPSTREAM+PROB_FULL] PASS' in cp.stdout and '"TOTAL_PASS": true' in cp.stdout
  print('[FULL_INHERITED_MODEL]', 'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL')
  if not ok: print(cp.stdout[-10000:]); print(cp.stderr[-4000:]); raise SystemExit(2)
  r=run(); print(json.dumps(_jsonable(r),indent=2,default=str)); raise SystemExit(0 if r['TOTAL_PASS'] else 1)
~~~

\==== 文件结束：intent\_stress\_l5\_rnd\_v14.py

## 附录 B　运行命令与完整结果

运行环境：Python 3.13.16，Linux。十个源码放在只含这十个文件的独立目录 src/ 中，从该目录之外以各文件的实际路径启动，没有建立任何外部目录链接。运行命令：

&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;for p in fldb\_work.py probability\_on\_fldb\_rnd.py probability\_on\_fldb\_rnd\_v2.py \\  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;intent\_on\_fldb\_rnd\_v14.py intent\_judge\_matrix\_rnd\_v14.py intent\_interface\_matrix\_rnd\_v14.py \\  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;intent\_idyn\_interface\_matrix\_rnd\_v14.py intent\_limit\_matrix\_rnd\_v14.py \\  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;intent\_process\_witness\_rnd\_v14.py intent\_stress\_l5\_rnd\_v14.py; do  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 python3 src/\$p \> out/\${p%.py}.out 2\>&1; echo "\$p rc=\$?"  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;done

运行时长：在双核 Linux 上，十个程序依次运行合计约两分钟，单个程序最长约半分钟。每个程序启动时先以子进程重跑它的上游程序作自检（第八节），所以总时长大于各程序自身检验的时长；嵌套重跑的限时为 180 至 300 秒，远大于实际所需。运行环境对单条命令限时的，可按上面的命令逐个运行，再逐份与下文比对，判据不变。

十个程序返回码全部为 0。各程序的标准输出与标准错误合并收录如下，未删节。多次独立运行的输出除以下两处外逐字节相同：一是形如“intent-fldb-\<数字\>”与“prob-fldb-\<数字\>”的运行对象标识，它取自 Python 对象地址，每次运行不同；二是打印出的冻结集合（frozenset）中元素的先后次序，它随 Python 的字符串散列种子而变。两者都只出现在打印的观察记录中，不进入任何检验；以不同散列种子运行，检验结果与集合内容完全相同。

2\. 复现器

复现器只用 Python 3 标准库。它从本式文中取出附录 A 的十个源码并核对 SHA-256，取出附录 B 的记录输出，在工作目录下按依赖次序运行十个程序，把每份输出与记录逐字比对（只把运行对象标识统一替换、把冻结集合的元素按序排列后再比），最后打印复现结论；全部一致时返回码为 0。  
reproduce\_p16.py　62 行　3227 字节  
SHA-256：d6b8426977a7a6a95db2c1e3d09ecd158d849880384edf61d966ba024b89b488  
\==== 文件开始：reproduce\_p16.py

~~~python
# 意向组式第十六 v1.0 复现器：只需本式文一个文件与 Python 3 标准库。
# 用法：python3 reproduce_p16.py <式文文件路径> [工作目录，缺省为 ./p16_reproduce]
import sys, re, hashlib, subprocess, os
from pathlib import Path

ORDER = ["fldb_work.py", "probability_on_fldb_rnd.py", "probability_on_fldb_rnd_v2.py",
         "intent_on_fldb_rnd_v14.py", "intent_judge_matrix_rnd_v14.py",
         "intent_interface_matrix_rnd_v14.py", "intent_idyn_interface_matrix_rnd_v14.py",
         "intent_limit_matrix_rnd_v14.py", "intent_process_witness_rnd_v14.py",
         "intent_stress_l5_rnd_v14.py"]
# 运行对象标识取自 Python 对象地址，每次运行不同，比对前统一替换。
ID = re.compile(r"(intent|prob)-fldb-\d+")
# 冻结集合打印时的元素次序随字符串散列种子而变，比对前按元素排序。
FS = re.compile(r"frozenset\(\{([^}]*)\}\)")

def norm(t):
    t = ID.sub(lambda m: m.group(1) + "-fldb-#", t.replace("\r\n", "\n").rstrip("\n"))
    return FS.sub(lambda m: "frozenset({" + ", ".join(sorted(x.strip() for x in m.group(1).split(","))) + "})", t)

def main():
    if len(sys.argv) < 2:
        print("用法：python3 reproduce_p16.py <式文文件路径> [工作目录]"); return 2
    text = Path(sys.argv[1]).read_text(encoding="utf-8").replace("\r\n", "\n")
    work = Path(sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else "p16_reproduce").resolve()
    src = work / "src"; out = work / "out"
    src.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True); out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    ok = True

    # 一、取出源码并核对 SHA-256
    files = {m.group(2): (m.group(1), m.group(3)) for m in re.finditer(
        r"SHA-256：([0-9a-f]{64})\n==== 文件开始：(\S+)\n(.*?)\n==== 文件结束：\2", text, re.S)}
    for p in ORDER:
        if p not in files:
            print(f"[缺失] {p}"); ok = False; continue
        want, body = files[p]
        data = (body + "\n").encode("utf-8")
        (src / p).write_bytes(data)
        got = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
        print(f"[SHA {'一致' if got == want else '不一致'}] {p}")
        ok &= (got == want)
    if not ok:
        print("源码取出或 SHA-256 核对未通过，停止。"); return 1

    # 二、取出附录 B 记录的输出
    recorded = {m.group(1): m.group(2) for m in re.finditer(
        r"==== 输出开始：(\S+)\n(.*?)\n==== 输出结束：\1", text, re.S)}

    # 三、按依赖次序运行并逐字比对
    env = dict(os.environ, PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE="1")
    for p in ORDER:
        cp = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(src / p)], stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
                            stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, text=True, env=env, cwd=str(work))
        (out / (p[:-3] + ".out")).write_text(cp.stdout, encoding="utf-8")
        same = (p in recorded) and norm(cp.stdout) == norm(recorded[p])
        print(f"[运行] {p}　返回码 {cp.returncode}　与附录 B {'一致' if same else '不一致'}")
        ok &= (cp.returncode == 0 and same)

    print("复现结论：" + ("全部一致，十个程序返回码均为 0。" if ok else "存在不一致，见上。"))
    return 0 if ok else 1

if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
~~~

\==== 文件结束：reproduce\_p16.py
